>> # Fast folder walker — skips excluded dirs WITHOUT entering them
>> function Get-FilesFast([string]$path) {
>>     $stack = [System.Collections.Generic.Stack[string]]::new()
>>     $stack.Push($path)
>> 
>>     while ($stack.Count -gt 0) {
>>         $dir = $stack.Pop()
>>         try {
>>             foreach ($sub in [System.IO.Directory]::EnumerateDirectories($dir)) {
>>                 $name = [System.IO.Path]::GetFileName($sub)
>>                 if ($skipDirs -notcontains $name) {
>>                     $stack.Push($sub)
>>                 }
>>             }
>>             foreach ($file in [System.IO.Directory]::EnumerateFiles($dir)) {
>>                 $ext = [System.IO.Path]::GetExtension($file).ToLower()
>>                 if ($exts -contains $ext -and $file -notlike "*$outFile") {
>>                     $file
>>                 }
>>             }
>>         } catch {
>>             # skip inaccessible dirs
>>         }
>>     }
>> }
>> 
>> # Stream straight to file (no memory buildup)
>> $sw = [System.IO.StreamWriter]::new((Join-Path (Get-Location) $outFile), $false, [System.Text.Encoding]::UTF8)
>> $count = 0
>> 
>> try {
>>     $sw.WriteLine("COMBINED CODE — $(Get-Date)")
>>     $sw.WriteLine("=" * 80)
>>     $sw.WriteLine("")
>> 
>>     foreach ($file in (Get-FilesFast (Get-Location).Path)) {
>>         $count++
>>         Write-Host "  [$count] $file"
>>         $sw.WriteLine("=" * 80)
>>         $sw.WriteLine("FILE: $file")
>>         $sw.WriteLine("=" * 80)
>>         try {
>>             $sw.Write([System.IO.File]::ReadAllText($file))
>>         } catch {
>>             $sw.WriteLine("[ERROR reading file]")

>>         $sw.WriteLine("")
>>         $sw.WriteLine("")
>>     }
>> }
>> finally {
>>     $sw.Close()

require("dotenv").config();

const express = require("express");
const path = require("path");

const connectDB = require("./config/database");
const routes = require("./routes");

const app = express();
const eyeTrackingRoutes = require("./routes/eyeTracking");
const eyeAssessmentRoutes = require("./routes/eyeAssessment");

app.set("view engine", "ejs");
app.set("views", path.join(__dirname, "views"));

app.use(express.urlencoded({
    extended: true,
    limit: "20kb"
}));

app.use(express.json({ limit: "5mb" }));

app.use(express.static(path.join(__dirname, "public")));

app.use("/", eyeTrackingRoutes);
app.use("/", eyeAssessmentRoutes);
app.use("/", routes);

app.use((req, res) => {
    res.status(404).send("Page not found");
});

const PORT = process.env.PORT || 3000;

const startServer = async () => {
    await connectDB();

    app.listen(PORT, () => {
        console.log(`Server running on port ${PORT}`);
    });
};

startServer();

# BrainGuard AI – Eye Assessment Monitoring (Research Prototype)

BrainGuard AI collects a self-reported health profile, then guides each assessment session through four eye-tracking tasks and produces one consolidated report.

> This is a research prototype, not a medical device. It is not clinically validated and must not be used to diagnose, screen for, or rule out any condition. Measurements can be affected by lighting, camera position, head movement, glasses, and chance. Contact a qualified healthcare professional about health concerns.

## User workflow

**Home → Health Profile → Eye Assessment → Saccadic → Smooth Pursuit → Blink and Fatigue → Memory-Based Eye Tracking → Consolidated Report → Dashboard / Assessment History**

The health profile and eye-test results are saved in MongoDB under the same assessment session. The server enforces the order and only renders the consolidated report after all four results have completed successfully.

## Architecture

```
Browser ──► Express app (Node.js, port 3000) ──► MongoDB Atlas
   │
   └──────► GazeMap service (FastAPI, port 8001): gaze tracking and eye-test analysis
Browser ◄──WebSocket── GazeMap service (/ws/gaze, live gaze data)
```

`app.js`, `routes/`, `models/`, `views/`, and `public/` contain the Express application and eye-assessment interface. `GazeMap/gaze_service.py` provides gaze estimation and analysis endpoints.

## Run locally

Requirements: Node.js 20+, Python, a MongoDB database configured with `MONGODB_URI`, and a webcam.

### 1. Start the Express application

```bash
npm install
npm start
```

Open <http://localhost:3000>.

### 2. Start the GazeMap service

Use a separate virtual environment because GazeMap installs PyTorch, OpenCV, and MediaPipe.

```bash
cd GazeMap
python -m venv .venv
.venv\Scripts\pip install -r requirements.txt
.venv\Scripts\python -m uvicorn gaze_service:app --host 127.0.0.1 --port 8001
```

Set `GAZE_API_URL` and `BRAINGUARD_ORIGINS` if the defaults are not appropriate for your setup. Unless explicitly set, `GAZE_WS_URL` follows the `GAZE_API_URL` host and uses `/ws/gaze`. GazeMap model files and the MediaPipe `face_landmarker.task` file may be downloaded on first run; see `GazeMap/README.md` if automatic downloads fail.

The browser opens the GazeMap WebSocket only after you start an assessment and grant camera permission. If the connection fails, confirm that GazeMap is running on the configured URL and that `BRAINGUARD_ORIGINS` includes the exact site origin (by default, `http://localhost:3000` and `http://127.0.0.1:3000`).

After all four eye assessments are complete, the consolidated report page offers a downloadable PDF containing the health profile, measurements, calibration and tracking quality, observations, and limitations.

## Eye assessments

All four tasks run in the browser with on-page calibration where applicable. In this flow, the FastAPI service processes browser-captured frames and does not open a second camera window.

- **Saccadic:** 12 center-to-side target trials; reports measured gaze, target acquisition, and response timing when sample quality supports them.
- **Smooth pursuit:** a 30-second moving-target task with five tracking cycles.
- **Blink and fatigue:** approximately 30 seconds of eye-openness sampling and blink/closure analysis.
- **Memory-based tracking:** eight encoding words, a five-second retention interval, and 16 recognition trials with gaze samples.

These are experimental observations, not clinically validated measurements.

## Data and limitations

- Each assessment is linked to its health profile and eye results through an assessment ID; history is grouped by email.
- Accounts and authentication are not implemented. Do not expose this prototype to the internet or use it to store real health data until authentication, consent, and privacy protections are added.
- Webcam gaze accuracy depends on lighting, camera, glasses, and head pose. Comparisons are meaningful only under similar conditions.
- Eye movement and blink measurements are experimental observations, not confirmed medical findings.
- The GazeMap trained model weights are derived from MPIIGaze and are licensed CC BY-NC-SA 4.0 for non-commercial use only.

## Credits and licences

- GazeMap by Fuad Mammadov: <https://github.com/fammad/GazeMap>, included under Apache License 2.0 (see `GazeMap/LICENSE` and `GazeMap/NOTICE`).
- MediaPipe provides face landmarks.

BrainGuard's own code does not yet have a licence file; add one before accepting contributions or reuse.

<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/chart.js"></script>
<script id="eye-report-data" type="application/json"><%- JSON.stringify(reportSections.map(section => ({ type: section.type, samples: section.samples }))).replace(/</g, "\\u003c") %></script>
<script>
    const reportData = JSON.parse(document.getElementById("eye-report-data").textContent);

    for (const section of reportData) {
        const canvas = document.getElementById(`report-chart-${section.type}`);
        if (!canvas) continue;

        const samples = section.samples;
        const labels = samples.map((sample, index) =>
            sample.timeSeconds ?? sample.time ?? index
        );
        const datasets = section.type === "blink_monitoring"
            ? [
                { label: "Left eye EAR", data: samples.map(sample => sample.leftEAR), borderColor: "#3b82f6" },
                { label: "Right eye EAR", data: samples.map(sample => sample.rightEAR), borderColor: "#f59e0b" },
                { label: "Average EAR", data: samples.map(sample => sample.avgEAR), borderColor: "#10b981" },
            ]
            : [
                { label: "Gaze X (px)", data: samples.map(sample => sample.valid ? sample.xPx : null), borderColor: "#3b82f6" },
                { label: "Gaze Y (px)", data: samples.map(sample => sample.valid ? sample.yPx : null), borderColor: "#10b981" },
                { label: "Target X (px)", data: samples.map(sample => sample.targetXPx ?? null), borderColor: "#ef4444" },
            ];

        new Chart(canvas, {
            type: "line",
            data: {
                labels,
                datasets: datasets.map(dataset => ({
                    ...dataset,
                    pointRadius: 0,
                    borderWidth: 1.5,
                    spanGaps: false,
                })),
            },
            options: {
                responsive: true,
                maintainAspectRatio: false,
                scales: {
                    x: { title: { display: true, text: "Sample time (seconds, where available)" } },
                    y: { title: { display: true, text: section.type === "blink_monitoring" ? "Eye aspect ratio" : "Position (pixels)" } },
                },
            },
        });
    }
</script>

<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/chart.js"></script>
<div
    id="eyeResultData"
    data-samples="<%= JSON.stringify(result.samples || []) %>"
    data-blink-events="<%= JSON.stringify(result.blinkEvents || []) %>"
    data-closure-events="<%= JSON.stringify(result.closureEvents || []) %>"
></div>
<script>
    const resultData = document.getElementById("eyeResultData").dataset;
    const samples = JSON.parse(resultData.samples);
    const blinkEvents = JSON.parse(resultData.blinkEvents);
    const closureEvents = JSON.parse(resultData.closureEvents);

    // Chart 1 — openness over time
    const openSamples = samples.filter(s => s.valid && (s.leftEAR != null || s.rightEAR != null));
    new Chart(document.getElementById("opennessChart"), {
        type: "line",
        data: {
            datasets: [
                {
                    label: "Left eye EAR",
                    data: openSamples.map(s => ({ x: s.timeSeconds, y: s.leftEAR })),
                    borderColor: "#3b82f6",
                    backgroundColor: "rgba(59,130,246,0.15)",
                    showLine: true,
                    borderWidth: 1,
                    pointRadius: 0,
                    tension: 0.2,
                },
                {
                    label: "Right eye EAR",
                    data: openSamples.map(s => ({ x: s.timeSeconds, y: s.rightEAR })),
                    borderColor: "#f59e0b",
                    backgroundColor: "rgba(245,158,11,0.15)",
                    showLine: true,
                    borderWidth: 1,
                    pointRadius: 0,
                    tension: 0.2,
                },
            ],
        },
        options: {
            responsive: true,
            maintainAspectRatio: false,
            scales: {
                x: { type: "linear", title: { display: true, text: "Time (s)" } },
                y: { title: { display: true, text: "EAR" } },
            },
        },
    });

    // Chart 2 — blink duration distribution
    const durations = blinkEvents.map(e => e.durationMs);
    new Chart(document.getElementById("durationChart"), {
        type: "bar",
        data: {
            labels: blinkEvents.map((_, i) => `Blink ${i + 1}`),
            datasets: [{
                label: "Duration (ms)",
                data: durations,
                backgroundColor: "#10b981",
                borderRadius: 4,
            }],
        },
        options: {
            responsive: true,
            maintainAspectRatio: false,
            scales: {
                x: { title: { display: true, text: "Blink event" } },
                y: { beginAtZero: true, title: { display: true, text: "Duration (ms)" } },
            },
        },
    });

    // Chart 3 — closure timeline
    new Chart(document.getElementById("closureChart"), {
        type: "scatter",
        data: {
            datasets: closureEvents.map(e => ({
                label: e.category,
                data: [{ x: e.startTimeSeconds, y: 1 }],
                backgroundColor:
                    e.category === "prolonged_closure" ? "#dc2626" :
                    e.category === "longer_closure" ? "#f59e0b" :
                    e.category === "uncertain_incomplete" ? "#6b7280" :
                    "#3b82f6",
                pointRadius: 8,
            })),
        },
        options: {
            responsive: true,
            maintainAspectRatio: false,
            scales: {
                x: { type: "linear", title: { display: true, text: "Time (s)" } },
                y: { display: false, min: 0, max: 2 },
            },
            plugins: {
                tooltip: {
                    callbacks: {
                        label: ctx => `${ctx.dataset.label} at ${ctx.parsed.x.toFixed(2)}s`,
                    },
                },
            },
        },
    });

    // Chart 4 — blinks per 5-second interval
    const intervalSec = 5;
    const totalDuration = samples.length > 0 ? samples[samples.length - 1].timeSeconds : 30;
    const numIntervals = Math.ceil(totalDuration / intervalSec);
    const intervalCounts = new Array(numIntervals).fill(0);
    blinkEvents.forEach(e => {
        const idx = Math.floor(e.startTimeSeconds / intervalSec);
        if (idx >= 0 && idx < numIntervals) intervalCounts[idx] += 1;
    });

    new Chart(document.getElementById("intervalChart"), {
        type: "bar",
        data: {
            labels: intervalCounts.map((_, i) => `${i * intervalSec}-${(i + 1) * intervalSec}s`),
            datasets: [{
                label: "Blinks detected",
                data: intervalCounts,
                backgroundColor: "#6366f1",
                borderRadius: 4,
            }],
        },
        options: {
            responsive: true,
            maintainAspectRatio: false,
            scales: {
                x: { title: { display: true, text: "Time interval" } },
                y: { beginAtZero: true, title: { display: true, text: "Blinks" } },
            },
        },
    });
</script>
<%- include("partials/footer") %>

const test = require("node:test");
const assert = require("node:assert/strict");

const {
    ASSESSMENT_STEPS,
    getCompletedTypes,
    getNextAssessment,
} = require("../services/assessmentWorkflow");

test("eye assessments unlock in the required order", () => {
    assert.deepEqual(
        ASSESSMENT_STEPS.map(step => step.type),
        ["saccadic", "smooth_pursuit", "blink_monitoring", "memory_tracking"]
    );
    const completed = new Set();

    for (const step of ASSESSMENT_STEPS) {
        assert.equal(getNextAssessment(completed).type, step.type);
        completed.add(step.type);
    }

    assert.equal(getNextAssessment(completed), null);
});

test("later completed tests do not skip an incomplete earlier test", () => {
    const completed = new Set(["smooth_pursuit", "memory_tracking"]);

    assert.equal(getNextAssessment(completed).type, "saccadic");
});

test("legacy attention results do not count toward the current workflow", () => {
    const completed = getCompletedTypes([
        "attention",
        "smooth_pursuit",
        "memory_tracking",
    ]);

    assert.deepEqual([...completed], ["smooth_pursuit", "memory_tracking"]);
    assert.equal(getNextAssessment(completed).type, "saccadic");
});

const assert = require("node:assert/strict");
const test = require("node:test");

const createEyeAssessmentPdf = require("../services/eyeAssessmentPdf");

test("creates a multipage-ready PDF for all completed eye assessments", async () => {
    const pdf = createEyeAssessmentPdf({
        assessment: { name: "Test participant" },
        healthProfile: {
            age: 30,
            language: "English",
            educationLevel: "College",
            sleepHours: 7,
            exerciseMinutes: 30,
            stressLevel: 2,
            familyHistory: "None",
        },
        reportSections: [
            {
                number: "01",
                type: "saccadic",
                title: "Saccadic Eye Movement Test",
                sessionId: "saccadic-session",
                startedAt: new Date("2026-01-01T10:00:00Z"),
                durationSeconds: 60,
                measurements: [{ label: "Total Samples", value: 100 }],
                calibration: [{ label: "Available", value: true }],
                quality: [{ label: "Valid Sample Fraction", value: 0.9 }],
                qualityNotes: ["Tracking was stable."],
                observations: ["Example observation."],
                samples: [
                    { timeSeconds: 1, xPx: 100, yPx: 80, targetXPx: 110, valid: true },
                    { timeSeconds: 2, xPx: 110, yPx: 90, targetXPx: 120, valid: true },
                ],
            },
            ...[
                { type: "smooth_pursuit", title: "Smooth Pursuit Test" },
                { type: "blink_monitoring", title: "Blink Monitoring Test" },
                { type: "memory_tracking", title: "Memory Tracking Test" },
            ].map(({ type, title }, index) => ({
                    number: String(index + 2).padStart(2, "0"),
                    type,
                    title,
                    sessionId: `session-${index + 2}`,
                    startedAt: new Date("2026-01-01T10:00:00Z"),
                    durationSeconds: 60,
                    measurements: [],
                    calibration: [],
                    quality: [],
                    qualityNotes: [],
                    observations: [],
                    samples: type === "blink_monitoring"
                        ? [
                            { timeSeconds: 1, leftEAR: 0.3, rightEAR: 0.31, avgEAR: 0.305 },
                            { timeSeconds: 2, leftEAR: 0.28, rightEAR: 0.29, avgEAR: 0.285 },
                        ]
                        : [],
                })),
        ],
        heatmaps: [{
            stimulus: "sample-image.jpg",
            sampleCount: 20,
            heatmapFilename: "not-present.png",
            createdAt: new Date("2026-01-01T10:00:00Z"),
        }],
    });
    const chunks = [];
    const completed = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        pdf.on("data", chunk => chunks.push(chunk));
        pdf.on("end", () => resolve(Buffer.concat(chunks)));
        pdf.on("error", reject);
    });

    assert.equal(pdf.info.Title, "Consolidated Eye Assessment Report");
    pdf.end();
    const output = await completed;

    assert.equal(output.subarray(0, 5).toString("ascii"), "%PDF-");
    assert.ok(output.length > 1000);
    assert.ok(output.toString("latin1").includes("%%EOF"));
});

FILE: C:\Users\surendra purohit\Documents\clean\test\gaze-socket.test.js
================================================================================
const assert = require("node:assert/strict");
const fs = require("node:fs");
const path = require("node:path");
const test = require("node:test");
const vm = require("node:vm");

const source = fs.readFileSync(
    path.join(__dirname, "..", "public", "js", "eye-tests", "gaze-socket.js"),
    "utf8"
);

class FakeWebSocket {
    static CONNECTING = 0;
    static OPEN = 1;
    static CLOSED = 3;
    static instances = [];

    constructor(url) {
        this.url = url;
        this.readyState = FakeWebSocket.CONNECTING;
        this.listeners = new Map();
        this.sent = [];
        FakeWebSocket.instances.push(this);
    }

    addEventListener(type, listener, options = {}) {
        const listeners = this.listeners.get(type) || [];
        listeners.push({ listener, once: options.once === true });
        this.listeners.set(type, listeners);
    }

    dispatch(type, event = {}) {
        const listeners = this.listeners.get(type) || [];
        this.listeners.set(type, listeners.filter(entry => !entry.once));
        listeners.forEach(entry => entry.listener(event));
    }

    open() {
        this.readyState = FakeWebSocket.OPEN;
        this.dispatch("open");
    }

    send(message) {
        this.sent.push(message);
    }

    receive(message) {
        this.dispatch("message", { data: JSON.stringify(message) });
    }

    close(code = 1000, reason = "") {
        if (this.readyState === FakeWebSocket.CLOSED) return;
        this.readyState = FakeWebSocket.CLOSED;
        this.dispatch("close", { code, reason });
    }
}

function createClient(url, options = {}) {
    FakeWebSocket.instances = [];
    const window = {
        WebSocket: FakeWebSocket,
        setTimeout,
        clearTimeout,
    };
    vm.runInNewContext(source, { window });
    return window.GazeSocket.create(url, options);
}

test("creating a GazeMap client does not open a WebSocket", () => {
    const client = createClient("ws://127.0.0.1:8001/ws/gaze");
    assert.equal(FakeWebSocket.instances.length, 0);
    client.close();
    assert.equal(FakeWebSocket.instances.length, 0);
});

test("all four eye assessments connect only after camera access and session start", () => {
    const assessments = [
        {
            page: "eye-saccadic.ejs",
            script: "saccadic.js",
            start: "async function startTest()",
            connect: "await initializeGazeMap()",
        },
        {
            page: "eye-smooth-pursuit.ejs",
            script: "smooth-pursuit.js",
            start: "async function startTest()",
            connect: "await initializeGazeMap()",
        },
        {
            page: "eye-memory-tracking.ejs",
            script: "memory-tracking.js",
            start: "async function startTest()",
            connect: "await initializeGazeMap()",
        },
        {
            page: "eye-blink-monitoring.ejs",
            script: "blink-monitoring.js",
            start: "startButton.addEventListener(\"click\", async () => {",
            connect: "await connectSocket()",
        },
    ];

    for (const assessment of assessments) {
        const page = fs.readFileSync(
            path.join(__dirname, "..", "views", assessment.page),
            "utf8"
        );
        const script = fs.readFileSync(
            path.join(
                __dirname,
                "..",
                "public",
                "js",
                "eye-tests",
                assessment.script
            ),
            "utf8"
        );
        const startIndex = script.indexOf(assessment.start);
        const cameraIndex = script.indexOf("getUserMedia", startIndex);
        const connectIndex = script.indexOf(assessment.connect, startIndex);
        const sessionStartIndex = script.indexOf("await requestJson(", cameraIndex);

        assert.match(page, /\/js\/eye-tests\/gaze-socket\.js/);
        assert.match(script, /window\.GazeSocket\.create\(websocketUrl/);
        assert.match(script, /Still waiting for camera permission/);
        assert.match(script, /Camera permission granted/);
        assert.ok(startIndex >= 0, `${assessment.script} has a start handler`);
        assert.ok(cameraIndex > startIndex, `${assessment.script} requests camera`);
        assert.ok(sessionStartIndex > cameraIndex, `${assessment.script} starts a session`);
        assert.ok(connectIndex > sessionStartIndex, `${assessment.script} connects after session start`);
    }
});

test("connect opens once and matches replies to their request", async () => {
    const client = createClient("ws://127.0.0.1:8001/ws/gaze");
    const connecting = client.connect();
    const sameConnection = client.connect();
    assert.equal(FakeWebSocket.instances.length, 1);
    assert.equal(FakeWebSocket.instances[0].url, "ws://127.0.0.1:8001/ws/gaze");

    const socket = FakeWebSocket.instances[0];
    socket.open();
    await Promise.all([connecting, sameConnection]);

    const responsePromise = client.request(
        "init",
        { widthPx: 1280, heightPx: 720 },
        "initialized"
    );
    const request = JSON.parse(socket.sent[0]);
    assert.equal(request.type, "init");
    assert.equal(request.widthPx, 1280);
    socket.receive({
        type: "initialized",
        requestId: request.requestId,
        widthPx: 1280,
    });

    const response = await responsePromise;
    assert.equal(response.type, "initialized");
    client.close();
});

test("intentional close rejects pending requests without a disconnect callback", async () => {
    const closeErrors = [];
    const client = createClient("ws://127.0.0.1:8001/ws/gaze", {
        onClose: error => closeErrors.push(error),
    });
    const connecting = client.connect();
    const socket = FakeWebSocket.instances[0];
    socket.open();
    await connecting;

    const responsePromise = client.request("frame");
    const rejection = assert.rejects(
        responsePromise,
        /browser camera session has ended/
    );
    client.close();

    await rejection;
    assert.deepEqual(closeErrors, []);
});

test("unexpected disconnect rejects pending requests and notifies the owner", async () => {
    const closeErrors = [];
    const client = createClient("ws://127.0.0.1:8001/ws/gaze", {
        onClose: error => closeErrors.push(error.message),
    });
    const connecting = client.connect();
    const socket = FakeWebSocket.instances[0];
    socket.open();
    await connecting;

    const responsePromise = client.request("frame");
    const rejection = assert.rejects(responsePromise, /service stopped/);
    socket.close(1011, "service stopped");

    await rejection;
    assert.deepEqual(closeErrors, ["service stopped"]);
});

test("failed connection reports service and origin configuration guidance", async () => {
    const client = createClient("ws://127.0.0.1:8001/ws/gaze");
    const connecting = client.connect();
    const socket = FakeWebSocket.instances[0];
    const rejection = assert.rejects(connecting, /BRAINGUARD_ORIGINS/);

    socket.dispatch("error");

    await rejection;
    assert.equal(socket.readyState, FakeWebSocket.CLOSED);
});

================================================================================
const HealthProfile = require("../models/HealthProfile");
const EyeAssessmentResult = require("../models/EyeAssessmentResult");
const BlinkMonitoringResult = require("../models/BlinkMonitoringResult");
const MemoryEyeTracking = require("../models/MemoryEyeTracking");

const ASSESSMENT_STEPS = [
    { type: "saccadic", path: "saccadic", title: "Saccadic Eye Movement Test" },
    { type: "smooth_pursuit", path: "smooth-pursuit", title: "Smooth Pursuit Test" },
    { type: "blink_monitoring", path: "blink-monitoring", title: "Blink and Fatigue Monitoring Test" },
    { type: "memory_tracking", path: "memory-tracking", title: "Memory-Based Eye Tracking Test" },
];

function getCompletedTypes(assessmentTypes) {
    const activeTypes = new Set(ASSESSMENT_STEPS.map(step => step.type));
    return new Set(assessmentTypes.filter(type => activeTypes.has(type)));
}

function getNextAssessment(completedTypes) {
    return ASSESSMENT_STEPS.find(step => !completedTypes.has(step.type)) || null;
}

async function getAssessmentProgress(assessmentId) {
    const [healthProfile, results, blinkResult, memoryResult] = await Promise.all([
        HealthProfile.findOne({ assessmentId }).select("_id").lean(),
        EyeAssessmentResult.find({
            assessment: assessmentId,
            status: "completed",
        }).select("assessmentType").lean(),
        BlinkMonitoringResult.findOne({
            assessment: assessmentId,
            status: "completed",
        }).select("_id").lean(),
        MemoryEyeTracking.findOne({
            assessment: assessmentId,
            status: "completed",
        }).select("_id").lean(),
    ]);

    const completedTypes = getCompletedTypes(
        results.map(result => result.assessmentType)
    );
    if (!blinkResult) completedTypes.delete("blink_monitoring");
    if (!memoryResult) completedTypes.delete("memory_tracking");

    return {
        profileComplete: Boolean(healthProfile),
        completedTypes,
        nextAssessment: getNextAssessment(completedTypes),
        complete: ASSESSMENT_STEPS.every(step => completedTypes.has(step.type)),
    };
}

module.exports = {
    ASSESSMENT_STEPS,
    getAssessmentProgress,
    getCompletedTypes,
    getNextAssessment,
};

const fs = require("node:fs");
const path = require("node:path");
const PDFDocument = require("pdfkit");

const PAGE_MARGIN = 52;
const FOOTER_SPACE = 48;
const HEATMAP_DIR = path.resolve(__dirname, "..", "GazeMap", "heatmaps");

function displayValue(value) {
    if (value === null || value === undefined || value === "") {
        return "Not recorded";
    }
    return String(value);
}

function displayDate(value) {
    if (!value) return "Unavailable";
    const date = new Date(value);
    return Number.isNaN(date.getTime())
        ? "Unavailable"
        : date.toLocaleString("en-IN");
}

function ensureSpace(document, height) {
    const bottom = document.page.height - document.page.margins.bottom;
    if (document.y + height > bottom - FOOTER_SPACE) {
        document.addPage();
    }
}

function addHeading(document, text, size = 12) {
    document.moveDown(0.5);
    document.font("Helvetica-Bold")
        .fontSize(size)
        .fillColor("#17324d")
        .text(text);
    document.moveDown(0.2);
}

function addEntries(document, title, entries) {
    if (!entries.length) return;

    addHeading(document, title, 11);
    for (const entry of entries) {
        document.font("Helvetica-Bold")
            .fontSize(9)
            .fillColor("#334155")
            .text(`${entry.label}: `, { continued: true });
        document.font("Helvetica")
            .fontSize(9)
            .fillColor("#334155")
            .text(displayValue(entry.value), { indent: 10, lineGap: 2 });
    }
}

function addNotes(document, title, notes) {
    if (!notes.length) return;

    addHeading(document, title, 11);
    for (const note of notes) {
        document.font("Helvetica")
            .fontSize(9)
            .fillColor("#334155")
            .text(`- ${displayValue(note)}`, { indent: 10, lineGap: 2 });
    }
}

function addSampleChart(document, section) {
    const samples = section.samples;
    const series = section.type === "blink_monitoring"
        ? [
            { label: "Left eye EAR", key: "leftEAR", color: "#3b82f6" },
            { label: "Right eye EAR", key: "rightEAR", color: "#f59e0b" },
            { label: "Average EAR", key: "avgEAR", color: "#10b981" },
        ]
        : [
            { label: "Gaze X", key: "xPx", color: "#3b82f6", validOnly: true },
            { label: "Gaze Y", key: "yPx", color: "#10b981", validOnly: true },
            { label: "Target X", key: "targetXPx", color: "#ef4444" },
        ];
    const xValues = samples.map((sample, index) => {
        const time = sample.timeSeconds ?? sample.time ?? index;
        return Number.isFinite(Number(time)) ? Number(time) : index;
    });
    const chartSeries = series.map(item => ({
        ...item,
        values: samples.map(sample => (
            (!item.validOnly || sample.valid)
            && Number.isFinite(sample[item.key])
                ? sample[item.key]
                : null
        )),
    })).filter(item => item.values.some(value => value !== null));
    const allValues = chartSeries.flatMap(item =>
        item.values.filter(value => value !== null)
    );
    if (!allValues.length) return;

    ensureSpace(document, 190);
    addHeading(document, "Recorded sample visualization", 11);

    const chartLeft = PAGE_MARGIN + 36;
    const chartTop = document.y + 26;
    const chartWidth = document.page.width - PAGE_MARGIN * 2 - 48;
    const chartHeight = 112;
    const chartBottom = chartTop + chartHeight;
    let minX = Math.min(...xValues);
    let maxX = Math.max(...xValues);
    let minY = Math.min(...allValues);
    let maxY = Math.max(...allValues);
    if (minX === maxX) maxX = minX + 1;
    if (minY === maxY) {
        minY -= 0.5;
        maxY += 0.5;
    }

    document.font("Helvetica")
        .fontSize(7)
        .fillColor("#64748b");
    for (let tick = 0; tick <= 4; tick += 1) {
        const y = chartTop + (chartHeight * tick) / 4;
        const value = maxY - ((maxY - minY) * tick) / 4;
        document.strokeColor("#e2e8f0")
            .lineWidth(0.5)
            .moveTo(chartLeft, y)
            .lineTo(chartLeft + chartWidth, y)
            .stroke();
        document.text(value.toFixed(2), PAGE_MARGIN, y - 4, {
            width: 30,
            align: "right",
            lineBreak: false,
        });
    }
    document.strokeColor("#94a3b8")
        .lineWidth(0.7)
        .moveTo(chartLeft, chartTop)
        .lineTo(chartLeft, chartBottom)
        .lineTo(chartLeft + chartWidth, chartBottom)
        .stroke();
    document.text(minX.toFixed(1), chartLeft, chartBottom + 4, {
        lineBreak: false,
    });
    document.text(maxX.toFixed(1), chartLeft + chartWidth - 30, chartBottom + 4, {
        width: 30,
        align: "right",
        lineBreak: false,
    });

    for (const item of chartSeries) {
        let previousPoint = null;
        document.strokeColor(item.color).lineWidth(1.2);
        for (let index = 0; index < item.values.length; index += 1) {
            const value = item.values[index];
            if (value === null) {
                previousPoint = null;
                continue;
            }
            const point = {
                x: chartLeft + ((xValues[index] - minX) / (maxX - minX)) * chartWidth,
                y: chartBottom - ((value - minY) / (maxY - minY)) * chartHeight,
            };
            if (previousPoint) {
                document.moveTo(previousPoint.x, previousPoint.y)
                    .lineTo(point.x, point.y)
                    .stroke();
            }
            previousPoint = point;
        }
    }

    const legendY = chartBottom + 18;
    let legendX = chartLeft;
    document.font("Helvetica").fontSize(7);
    for (const item of chartSeries) {
        document.fillColor(item.color).text("-", legendX, legendY, {
            lineBreak: false,
        });
        legendX += 12;
        document.fillColor("#475569").text(item.label, legendX, legendY, {
            lineBreak: false,
        });
        legendX += document.widthOfString(item.label) + 18;
    }
    document.y = legendY + 10;
}

function createEyeAssessmentPdf({
    assessment,
    healthProfile,
    reportSections,
    heatmaps,
}) {
    const document = new PDFDocument({
        size: "A4",
        margins: {
            top: PAGE_MARGIN,
            right: PAGE_MARGIN,
            bottom: PAGE_MARGIN + FOOTER_SPACE,
            left: PAGE_MARGIN,
        },
        bufferPages: true,
        info: {
            Title: "Consolidated Eye Assessment Report",
            Author: "BrainGuard AI",
            Subject: `Eye assessment report for ${assessment.name}`,
        },
    });

    document.font("Helvetica-Bold")
        .fontSize(20)
        .fillColor("#17324d")
        .text("BrainGuard AI", { align: "center" });
    document.moveDown(0.3);
    document.fontSize(15)
        .text("Consolidated Eye Assessment Report", { align: "center" });
    document.moveDown(0.6);
    document.font("Helvetica")
        .fontSize(11)
        .fillColor("#334155")
        .text(`Assessment for ${displayValue(assessment.name)}`, { align: "center" });
    document.fontSize(9)
        .fillColor("#64748b")
        .text(`Generated ${displayDate(new Date())}`, { align: "center" });
    document.moveDown(0.8);

    addHeading(document, "Session summary", 14);
    addEntries(document, "Health profile", [
        { label: "Age", value: healthProfile.age },
        { label: "Preferred language", value: healthProfile.language },
        { label: "Education", value: healthProfile.educationLevel },
        { label: "Average sleep", value: `${healthProfile.sleepHours} hours/day` },
        { label: "Physical activity", value: `${healthProfile.exerciseMinutes} minutes/day` },
        { label: "Self-reported stress", value: `${healthProfile.stressLevel} / 5` },
        { label: "Family history", value: healthProfile.familyHistory },
    ]);

    for (const section of reportSections) {
        ensureSpace(document, 140);
        addHeading(document, `${section.number} - ${section.title}`, 14);
        document.font("Helvetica")
            .fontSize(9)
            .fillColor("#475569")
            .text(`Result session: ${displayValue(section.sessionId)}`)
            .text(`Recorded: ${displayDate(section.startedAt)}`)
            .text(`Duration: ${section.durationSeconds == null
                ? "Unavailable"
                : `${section.durationSeconds} seconds`}`);

        addEntries(document, "Derived measurements", section.measurements);
        addEntries(document, "Calibration", section.calibration);
        addEntries(document, "Tracking quality", section.quality);
        addNotes(document, "Tracking quality notes", section.qualityNotes);
        addNotes(document, "Algorithm-generated observations", section.observations);

        addSampleChart(document, section);
    }

    if (heatmaps.length) {
        ensureSpace(document, 100);
        addHeading(document, "Available gaze heatmaps", 14);
        for (const heatmap of heatmaps) {
            ensureSpace(document, 300);
            document.font("Helvetica")
                .fontSize(9)
                .fillColor("#334155")
                .text(
                    `${displayValue(heatmap.stimulus)} - ${displayValue(heatmap.sampleCount)} samples - ${displayDate(heatmap.createdAt)}`,
                    { indent: 10, lineGap: 2 }
                );
            const filename = path.basename(displayValue(heatmap.heatmapFilename));
            const imagePath = path.join(HEATMAP_DIR, filename);
            if (filename !== "Not recorded" && fs.existsSync(imagePath)) {
                document.image(imagePath, {
                    fit: [document.page.width - PAGE_MARGIN * 2, 250],
                    align: "center",
                });
                document.moveDown(0.5);
            } else {
                document.font("Helvetica-Oblique")
                    .fontSize(8)
                    .fillColor("#64748b")
                    .text("Heatmap image is not available on this server.", { indent: 10 });
            }
        }
    }

    ensureSpace(document, 100);
    addHeading(document, "Limitations and next steps", 14);
    document.font("Helvetica")
        .fontSize(9)
        .fillColor("#334155")
        .text(
            "Camera position, lighting, glasses, head movement, screen size, and tracking quality can affect results. Measurements are not clinically validated and cannot diagnose, screen for, or rule out neurological or other medical conditions."
        )
        .moveDown(0.4)
        .text(
            "If you have a health concern or persistent symptoms, discuss them with a qualified healthcare professional. Repeat measurements under similar conditions only as a descriptive personal comparison."
        )
        .moveDown(0.6)
        .font("Helvetica-Bold")
        .text(
            "BrainGuard AI is a research prototype, not a medical device. No result in this report is a confirmed medical finding."
        );

    const pages = document.bufferedPageRange();
    for (let pageIndex = pages.start; pageIndex < pages.start + pages.count; pageIndex += 1) {
        document.switchToPage(pageIndex);
        document.save();
        document.strokeColor("#dbe3ec")
            .lineWidth(0.5)
            .moveTo(PAGE_MARGIN, document.page.height - 38)
            .lineTo(document.page.width - PAGE_MARGIN, document.page.height - 38)
            .stroke();
        document.font("Helvetica")
            .fontSize(8)
            .fillColor("#64748b")
            .text(
                `BrainGuard AI - Page ${pageIndex + 1} of ${pages.count}`,
                PAGE_MARGIN,
                document.page.height - 28,
                { width: document.page.width - PAGE_MARGIN * 2, align: "center" }
            );
        document.restore();
    }

    return document;
}

module.exports = createEyeAssessmentPdf;

const express = require("express");
const mongoose = require("mongoose");
const crypto = require("crypto");

const Assessment = require("../models/Assessment");
const EyeAssessmentResult = require("../models/EyeAssessmentResult");
const BlinkMonitoringResult = require("../models/BlinkMonitoringResult");
const MemoryEyeTracking = require("../models/MemoryEyeTracking");
const EyeTracking = require("../models/EyeTracking");
const HealthProfile = require("../models/HealthProfile");
const {
    ASSESSMENT_STEPS,
    getAssessmentProgress,
} = require("../services/assessmentWorkflow");
const createEyeAssessmentPdf = require("../services/eyeAssessmentPdf");

const router = express.Router();
const GAZE_API = (
    process.env.GAZE_API_URL || "http://127.0.0.1:8001"
).replace(/\/+$/, "");
const defaultGazeWsUrl = new URL(GAZE_API);
defaultGazeWsUrl.protocol = defaultGazeWsUrl.protocol === "https:"
    ? "wss:"
    : "ws:";
defaultGazeWsUrl.username = "";
defaultGazeWsUrl.password = "";
defaultGazeWsUrl.pathname = "/ws/gaze";
defaultGazeWsUrl.search = "";
defaultGazeWsUrl.hash = "";
const GAZE_WS = process.env.GAZE_WS_URL || defaultGazeWsUrl.toString();
const assessmentTypeByPath = new Map([
    ["saccadic", "saccadic"],
    ["smooth-pursuit", "smooth_pursuit"],
    ["smooth_pursuit", "smooth_pursuit"],
    ["blink-monitoring", "blink_monitoring"],
    ["blink_monitoring", "blink_monitoring"],
    ["memory-tracking", "memory_tracking"],
    ["memory_tracking", "memory_tracking"],
]);
const stepCopy = {
    saccadic: {
        description: "Shift your gaze between a center fixation mark and side targets.",
        instructions: ["Allow camera access and complete calibration.", "Look at the center mark, then shift to each side target.", "Keep your head still."],
    },
    smooth_pursuit: {
        description: "Follow a moving target smoothly while calibrated gaze samples are recorded.",
        instructions: ["Allow camera access and complete calibration.", "Track the moving dot with your eyes.", "Keep your head still and remain in view."],
    },
