document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
    const runner = document.getElementById("blinkMonitoringTest");
    if (!runner) return;

    const assessmentId = runner.dataset.assessmentId;
    const websocketUrl = runner.dataset.gazeWsUrl;
    const startButton = document.getElementById("startBlinkTest");
    const cancelButton = document.getElementById("cancelBlinkTest");
    const statusText = document.getElementById("blinkStatus");
    const progressTrack = document.querySelector(".eye-progress-track");
    const progressFill = document.getElementById("blinkProgress");
    const progressText = document.getElementById("blinkProgressText");
    const stage = document.getElementById("blinkStage");
    const video = document.getElementById("blinkVideo");
    const signalCanvas = document.getElementById("blinkSignalCanvas");
    const eyePreviewCanvas = document.getElementById("blinkEyePreview");
    const readingParagraph = document.getElementById("blinkReadingParagraph");
    const earReadout = document.getElementById("blinkEarReadout");
    const cameraStatus = document.getElementById("blinkCameraStatus");
    const faceStatus = document.getElementById("blinkFaceStatus");

    const testDurationMs = 30_000;
    const frameIntervalMs = 85;
    const captureCanvas = document.createElement("canvas");
    const captureContext = captureCanvas.getContext("2d");
    const samples = [];
    let stream = null;
    let sessionId = null;
    let testStartedAt = 0;
    let latestLeftEyeLandmarks = null;
    let latestRightEyeLandmarks = null;
    let sampleTimer = null;
    let active = false;
    let finishing = false;
    let failureHandled = false;
    const gazeSocket = window.GazeSocket.create(websocketUrl, {
        onClose: error => {
            if (active) void failAssessment(error);
        },
    });

    function setProgress(value) {
        const progress = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Number(value) || 0));
        progressFill.style.width = `${progress}%`;
        progressText.textContent = `${Math.round(progress)}%`;
        progressTrack.setAttribute("aria-valuenow", String(Math.round(progress)));
    }

    function hasValidEyeLandmarks(landmarks) {
        return Array.isArray(landmarks)
            && landmarks.length === 16
            && landmarks.every(point =>
                Array.isArray(point)
                && point.length === 2
                && Number.isFinite(point[0])
                && Number.isFinite(point[1])
                && point[0] >= 0
                && point[0] <= 1
                && point[1] >= 0
                && point[1] <= 1
            );
    }

    function isEyeVisible(result, flag, landmarks, eyeRatio) {
        if (typeof result[flag] === "boolean") return result[flag];
        return hasValidEyeLandmarks(landmarks)
            && Number.isFinite(eyeRatio)
            && eyeRatio >= 0
            && eyeRatio <= 1;
    }

    async function requestJson(url, options = {}) {
        const response = await fetch(url, {
            ...options,
            headers: {
                "Content-Type": "application/json",
                ...options.headers,
            },
        });
        const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
        if (!response.ok || data.success === false) {
            throw new Error(data.message || `Request failed (${response.status}).`);
        }
        return data;
    }

    function connectSocket() {
        return gazeSocket.connect(8000);
    }

    function sendSocketRequest(type, data = {}) {
        return gazeSocket.request(type, data, type, 10000);
    }

    function closeSocket() {
        gazeSocket.close();
    }

    function stopCamera() {
        if (stream) {
            stream.getTracks().forEach(track => track.stop());
            stream = null;
        }
        video.srcObject = null;
        stage.classList.remove("eye-camera-active");
        cameraStatus.textContent = "Camera Off";
    }

    function releaseFullscreen() {
        if (document.fullscreenElement && document.exitFullscreen) {
            document.exitFullscreen().catch(() => {});
        }
    }

    function cleanup() {
        active = false;
        window.clearTimeout(sampleTimer);
        closeSocket();
        stopCamera();
        releaseFullscreen();
    }

    function drawSignal() {
        const rect = signalCanvas.getBoundingClientRect();
        if (!rect.width || !rect.height) return;
        const pixelRatio = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
        const canvasWidth = Math.round(rect.width * pixelRatio);
        const canvasHeight = Math.round(rect.height * pixelRatio);
        if (signalCanvas.width !== canvasWidth || signalCanvas.height !== canvasHeight) {
            signalCanvas.width = canvasWidth;
            signalCanvas.height = canvasHeight;
        }

        const context = signalCanvas.getContext("2d");
        context.setTransform(pixelRatio, 0, 0, pixelRatio, 0, 0);
        context.clearRect(0, 0, rect.width, rect.height);
        context.fillStyle = "#0b1220";
        context.fillRect(0, 0, rect.width, rect.height);
        context.strokeStyle = "rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.32)";
        context.lineWidth = 1;
        for (let row = 1; row < 4; row += 1) {
            const y = (rect.height * row) / 4;
            context.beginPath();
            context.moveTo(0, y);
            context.lineTo(rect.width, y);
            context.stroke();
        }

        const plotSamples = samples.slice(-180);
        const drawEye = (key, color) => {
            context.beginPath();
            context.strokeStyle = color;
            context.lineWidth = 2.5;
            let started = false;
            plotSamples.forEach((sample, index) => {
                const eyeVisible = key === "leftEAR"
                    ? sample.leftEyeVisible
                    : sample.rightEyeVisible;
                const value = eyeVisible && Number.isFinite(sample[key])
                    ? sample[key]
                    : null;
                if (value === null) {
                    started = false;
                    return;
                }
                const x = plotSamples.length <= 1
                    ? 0
                    : (index / (plotSamples.length - 1)) * rect.width;
                const y = rect.height - (Math.min(value, 0.8) / 0.8) * rect.height;
                if (started) context.lineTo(x, y);
                else {
                    context.moveTo(x, y);
                    started = true;
                }
            });
            context.stroke();
        };
        drawEye("leftEAR", "#60a5fa");
        drawEye("rightEAR", "#34d399");
    }

    function drawEyePreview(leftLandmarks, rightLandmarks) {
        const rect = eyePreviewCanvas.getBoundingClientRect();
        if (!rect.width || !rect.height) return;
        const pixelRatio = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
        const canvasWidth = Math.round(rect.width * pixelRatio);
        const canvasHeight = Math.round(rect.height * pixelRatio);
        if (eyePreviewCanvas.width !== canvasWidth || eyePreviewCanvas.height !== canvasHeight) {
            eyePreviewCanvas.width = canvasWidth;
            eyePreviewCanvas.height = canvasHeight;
        }

        const context = eyePreviewCanvas.getContext("2d");
        context.setTransform(pixelRatio, 0, 0, pixelRatio, 0, 0);
        context.fillStyle = "#0b1220";
        context.fillRect(0, 0, rect.width, rect.height);

        const visibleEyes = [
            { landmarks: leftLandmarks, color: "#38bdf8" },
            { landmarks: rightLandmarks, color: "#a3e635" },
        ].filter(eye => hasValidEyeLandmarks(eye.landmarks));
        if (visibleEyes.length === 0) {
            context.fillStyle = "#cbd5e1";
            context.font = "13px sans-serif";
            context.textAlign = "center";
            context.textBaseline = "middle";
            context.fillText("Eyes not visible. Look directly into camera.", rect.width / 2, rect.height / 2);
            return;
        }

        const allLandmarks = visibleEyes.flatMap(eye => eye.landmarks);
        const sourceWidth = captureCanvas.width;
        const sourceHeight = captureCanvas.height;
        const xs = allLandmarks.map(point => point[0] * sourceWidth);
        const ys = allLandmarks.map(point => point[1] * sourceHeight);
        const pointWidth = Math.max(...xs) - Math.min(...xs);
        const pointHeight = Math.max(...ys) - Math.min(...ys);
        const aspectRatio = rect.width / rect.height;
        let cropWidth = pointWidth + Math.max(24, pointWidth * 0.16);
        let cropHeight = pointHeight + Math.max(38, pointHeight * 1.8);
        if (cropWidth / cropHeight < aspectRatio) {
            cropWidth = cropHeight * aspectRatio;
        } else {
            cropHeight = cropWidth / aspectRatio;
        }
        cropWidth = Math.min(sourceWidth, cropWidth);
        cropHeight = Math.min(sourceHeight, cropHeight);
        const centerX = (Math.min(...xs) + Math.max(...xs)) / 2;
        const centerY = (Math.min(...ys) + Math.max(...ys)) / 2;
        const cropX = Math.max(0, Math.min(sourceWidth - cropWidth, centerX - cropWidth / 2));
        const cropY = Math.max(0, Math.min(sourceHeight - cropHeight, centerY - cropHeight / 2));

        context.drawImage(
            captureCanvas,
            cropX,
            cropY,
            cropWidth,
            cropHeight,
            0,
            0,
            rect.width,
            rect.height
        );

        const drawEye = (landmarks, color) => {
            const points = landmarks.map(point => ({
                x: ((point[0] * sourceWidth - cropX) / cropWidth) * rect.width,
                y: ((point[1] * sourceHeight - cropY) / cropHeight) * rect.height,
            }));
            context.save();
            context.strokeStyle = color;
            context.lineWidth = 2;
            context.shadowColor = color;
            context.shadowBlur = 4;
            context.beginPath();
            points.forEach((point, index) => {
                if (index === 0) context.moveTo(point.x, point.y);
                else context.lineTo(point.x, point.y);
            });
            context.closePath();
            context.stroke();
            context.shadowBlur = 0;
            points.forEach(point => {
                context.beginPath();
                context.arc(point.x, point.y, 2.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                context.fillStyle = "#f8fafc";
                context.fill();
                context.strokeStyle = color;
                context.stroke();
            });
            context.restore();
        };
        visibleEyes.forEach(eye => drawEye(eye.landmarks, eye.color));
    }

    function updateLiveReadout(sample) {
        const leftVisible = sample.leftEyeVisible === true;
        const rightVisible = sample.rightEyeVisible === true;
        if (!leftVisible && !rightVisible) {
            faceStatus.textContent =
                "Eyes not visible. Please look directly into the camera. Adjust your face or camera position.";
            earReadout.textContent = "--";
            return;
        }
        if (leftVisible !== rightVisible) {
            faceStatus.textContent =
                "Only one eye is detected. Center your face and adjust the camera so both eyes are visible.";
            earReadout.textContent = "--";
            return;
        }
        if (!sample.valid || !Number.isFinite(sample.avgEAR)) {
            faceStatus.textContent =
                "Eyes are not reliably tracked. Look directly into the camera and adjust your position.";
            earReadout.textContent = "--";
            return;
        }
        faceStatus.textContent = "Both eyes visible and tracked. Blink normally.";
        earReadout.textContent = sample.avgEAR.toFixed(3);
    }

    function encodeCaptureFrame() {
        if (!video.videoWidth || !video.videoHeight) {
            return Promise.reject(new Error("The browser camera has not produced a video frame yet."));
        }
        const scale = Math.min(1, 640 / video.videoWidth);
        const captureWidth = Math.max(1, Math.round(video.videoWidth * scale));
        const captureHeight = Math.max(1, Math.round(video.videoHeight * scale));
        if (captureCanvas.width !== captureWidth || captureCanvas.height !== captureHeight) {
            captureCanvas.width = captureWidth;
            captureCanvas.height = captureHeight;
        }
        captureContext.drawImage(video, 0, 0, captureCanvas.width, captureCanvas.height);
        return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
            captureCanvas.toBlob(blob => {
                if (!blob) {
                    reject(new Error("Could not encode the browser camera frame."));
                    return;
                }
                const reader = new FileReader();
                reader.onload = () => resolve(reader.result);
                reader.onerror = () => reject(new Error("Could not read the encoded camera frame."));
                reader.readAsDataURL(blob);
            }, "image/jpeg", 0.68);
        });
    }

    async function captureNextFrame() {
        if (!active) return;
        const captureTime = performance.now();
        const elapsedSeconds = Math.max(0, (captureTime - testStartedAt) / 1000);
        if (captureTime - testStartedAt >= testDurationMs) {
            await completeAssessment();
            return;
        }

        try {
            const imageBase64 = await encodeCaptureFrame();
            const result = await sendSocketRequest("frame", {
                imageBase64,
                timestampMs: Math.round(captureTime),
                includeEar: true,
            });
            const eyeValue = value => Number.isFinite(value) ? value : null;
            const leftEyeVisible = isEyeVisible(
                result,
                "leftEyeVisible",
                result.leftEyeLandmarks,
                result.leftEAR
            );
            const rightEyeVisible = isEyeVisible(
                result,
                "rightEyeVisible",
                result.rightEyeLandmarks,
                result.rightEAR
            );
            const sample = {
                timeSeconds: elapsedSeconds,
                leftEAR: eyeValue(result.leftEAR),
                rightEAR: eyeValue(result.rightEAR),
                avgEAR: eyeValue(result.avgEAR),
                faceDetected: result.faceDetected === true,
                leftEyeVisible,
                rightEyeVisible,
                valid: result.valid === true
                    && result.faceDetected === true
                    && leftEyeVisible
                    && rightEyeVisible
                    && Number.isFinite(result.leftEAR)
                    && Number.isFinite(result.rightEAR)
                    && Number.isFinite(result.avgEAR),
            };
            samples.push(sample);
            latestLeftEyeLandmarks = result.leftEyeLandmarks || null;
            latestRightEyeLandmarks = result.rightEyeLandmarks || null;
            updateLiveReadout(sample);
            drawEyePreview(result.leftEyeLandmarks, result.rightEyeLandmarks);
            drawSignal();

            const progress = ((performance.now() - testStartedAt) / testDurationMs) * 100;
            setProgress(progress);
            statusText.textContent = "Recording live eye-openness measurements. Blink naturally.";
            const delay = Math.max(0, frameIntervalMs - (performance.now() - captureTime));
            sampleTimer = window.setTimeout(captureNextFrame, delay);
        } catch (error) {
            if (active) await failAssessment(error);
        }
    }

    async function cancelServerSession(keepalive = false) {
        if (!sessionId) return;
        const cancellingSessionId = sessionId;
        sessionId = null;
        await requestJson(
            `/eye-assessment/${encodeURIComponent(assessmentId)}/blink-monitoring/browser/${encodeURIComponent(cancellingSessionId)}/cancel`,
            { method: "POST", keepalive }
        );
    }

    async function completeAssessment() {
        if (finishing || !sessionId) return;
        finishing = true;
        active = false;
        cancelButton.hidden = true;
        setProgress(100);
        statusText.textContent = "Analyzing the measured eye-openness samples...";
        closeSocket();
        stopCamera();
        releaseFullscreen();

        try {
            const result = await requestJson(
                `/eye-assessment/${encodeURIComponent(assessmentId)}/blink-monitoring/browser/${encodeURIComponent(sessionId)}/complete`,
                {
                    method: "POST",
                    body: JSON.stringify({
                        samples,
                        widthPx: Math.max(100, Math.round(window.innerWidth)),
                        heightPx: Math.max(100, Math.round(window.innerHeight)),
                    }),
                }
            );
            sessionId = null;
            statusText.textContent = "Blink monitoring complete. Opening your results...";
            if (result.resultUrl) window.location.assign(result.resultUrl);
        } catch (error) {
            finishing = false;
            await failAssessment(error);
        }
    }

    async function failAssessment(error) {
        if (failureHandled) return;
        failureHandled = true;
        cleanup();
        if (sessionId) {
            try {
                await cancelServerSession();
            } catch (cancelError) {
                console.error("Could not mark the blink session incomplete:", cancelError);
            }
        }
        statusText.textContent = `Unable to complete the test: ${error.message}`;
        cameraStatus.textContent = "Camera Off";
        cancelButton.hidden = true;
        startButton.disabled = false;
        runner.classList.remove("eye-test-active");
        stage.classList.remove("eye-test-active", "eye-camera-active");
        sessionId = null;
    }

    startButton.addEventListener("click", async () => {
        if (active) return;
        failureHandled = false;
        active = true;
        startButton.disabled = true;
        setProgress(0);
        statusText.textContent = "Requesting camera access...";
        cameraStatus.textContent = "Camera Off";
        faceStatus.textContent = "Waiting for camera.";
        samples.length = 0;
        readingParagraph.hidden = true;
        latestLeftEyeLandmarks = null;
        latestRightEyeLandmarks = null;
        drawEyePreview(null, null);
        drawSignal();

        try {
            if (
                document.fullscreenEnabled
                && typeof runner.requestFullscreen === "function"
                && !document.fullscreenElement
            ) {
                await runner.requestFullscreen();
            }
            if (!navigator.mediaDevices || !navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia) {
                throw new Error("Camera access requires a secure browser context (HTTPS or localhost).");
            }
            const permissionReminder = window.setTimeout(() => {
                if (active) {
                    statusText.textContent =
                        "Still waiting for camera permission. Allow access in the browser prompt or site settings.";
                }
            }, 10000);
            try {
                stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
                    audio: false,
                    video: {
                        facingMode: "user",
                        width: { ideal: 640 },
                        height: { ideal: 480 },
                    },
                });
            } finally {
                window.clearTimeout(permissionReminder);
            }
            if (!active) {
                stopCamera();
                return;
            }
            statusText.textContent = "Camera permission granted. Starting camera preview...";
            stream.getVideoTracks().forEach(track => {
                track.addEventListener("ended", () => {
                    cameraStatus.textContent = "Camera Off";
                    if (!active) return;
                    void failAssessment(new Error("Camera disconnected. Restart the test and grant camera access."));
                }, { once: true });
            });
            video.srcObject = stream;
            await video.play();
            if (!active) return;
            stage.classList.add("eye-test-active", "eye-camera-active");
            runner.classList.add("eye-test-active");
            statusText.textContent = "Camera ready. Starting assessment session...";
            cameraStatus.textContent = "Camera Active";
            faceStatus.textContent = "Connecting to GazeMap...";

            const session = await requestJson(
                `/eye-assessment/${encodeURIComponent(assessmentId)}/blink-monitoring/browser/start`,
                { method: "POST" }
            );
            sessionId = session.sessionId;
            if (!active) {
                await cancelServerSession(true);
                return;
            }
            cancelButton.hidden = false;
            cancelButton.disabled = false;

            statusText.textContent = "Assessment session ready. Connecting to GazeMap...";
            await connectSocket();
            if (!active) return;
            testStartedAt = performance.now();
            readingParagraph.hidden = false;
            statusText.textContent = "Read the passage naturally and blink as you normally would.";
            await captureNextFrame();
        } catch (error) {
            if (!active && !finishing) return;
            await failAssessment(error);
        }
    });

    cancelButton.addEventListener("click", async () => {
        if (!sessionId) return;
        cancelButton.disabled = true;
        cleanup();
        statusText.textContent = "Blink monitoring cancelled.";
        runner.classList.remove("eye-test-active");
        stage.classList.remove("eye-test-active", "eye-camera-active");
        cancelButton.hidden = true;
        startButton.disabled = false;
        try {
            await cancelServerSession();
        } catch (error) {
            statusText.textContent = `Unable to save cancellation: ${error.message}`;
        }
    });

    window.addEventListener("pagehide", () => {
        const shouldCancel = Boolean(sessionId);
        cleanup();
        if (shouldCancel) {
            void cancelServerSession(true).catch(error => {
                console.error("Could not cancel the blink session during page cleanup:", error);
            });
        }
    });

    drawEyePreview(null, null);
    window.addEventListener("resize", () => {
        drawSignal();
        drawEyePreview(latestLeftEyeLandmarks, latestRightEyeLandmarks);
    });
});

(() => {
    const targets = Object.freeze([
        Object.freeze([0.46, 0.06]),
        Object.freeze([0.7, 0.06]),
        Object.freeze([0.94, 0.06]),
        Object.freeze([0.06, 0.5]),
        Object.freeze([0.5, 0.5]),
        Object.freeze([0.94, 0.5]),
        Object.freeze([0.06, 0.94]),
        Object.freeze([0.5, 0.94]),
        Object.freeze([0.94, 0.94]),
    ]);

    function create(stage) {
        const panel = stage.querySelector("[data-calibration-panel]");
        const title = panel.querySelector("[data-calibration-title]");
        const targetText = panel.querySelector("[data-calibration-target]");
        const fill = panel.querySelector("[data-calibration-fill]");
        const progress = panel.querySelector("[data-calibration-progress]");
        const count = panel.querySelector("[data-calibration-count]");
        let totalTargets = targets.length;

        function setProgress(completed) {
            const percentage = Math.round((completed / totalTargets) * 100);
            fill.style.width = `${percentage}%`;
            progress.setAttribute("aria-valuenow", String(percentage));
            count.textContent = `${completed} of ${totalTargets} targets complete`;
        }

        return {
            targets,
            begin() {
                totalTargets = targets.length;
                panel.hidden = false;
                title.textContent = "Calibration starting";
                targetText.textContent =
                    "Keep your head still and follow each dot with your eyes. The test starts automatically after calibration.";
                setProgress(0);
            },
            showTarget(index, position) {
                const [x, y] = position;
                panel.hidden = false;
                title.textContent = `Calibration target ${index} of ${totalTargets}`;
                const horizontal = x < 0.34 ? "left" : x < 0.78 ? "center" : "right";
                const vertical = y < 0.48 ? "upper" : y < 0.78 ? "middle" : "lower";
                targetText.textContent =
                    `Look at the ${vertical} ${horizontal} dot (${Math.round(x * 100)}% from the left, ${Math.round(y * 100)}% from the top).`;
                setProgress(index - 1);
            },
            completeTarget(index) {
                setProgress(index);
            },
            fitting() {
                title.textContent = "Fitting gaze calibration";
                targetText.textContent =
                    "All targets captured. GazeMap is checking the calibration.";
                setProgress(totalTargets);
            },
            complete() {
                title.textContent = "Calibration complete";
                targetText.textContent = "Your test will begin automatically.";
                setProgress(totalTargets);
            },
            hide() {
                panel.hidden = true;
            },
        };
    }

    window.EyeCalibrationUI = { create };
})();

(function (global) {
    function createGazeSocket(url, options = {}) {
        let connection = null;
        let requestCounter = 0;
        const pendingRequests = new Map();

        function rejectPendingRequests(error) {
            for (const [requestId, pending] of pendingRequests) {
                global.clearTimeout(pending.timeout);
                pendingRequests.delete(requestId);
                pending.reject(error);
            }
        }

        function connect(timeoutMs = 12000) {
            if (connection?.socket.readyState === global.WebSocket.OPEN) {
                return Promise.resolve();
            }
            if (connection) return connection.promise;
            if (!url) {
                return Promise.reject(
                    new Error("The GazeMap WebSocket URL is not configured.")
                );
            }

            let socket;
            try {
                socket = new global.WebSocket(url);
            } catch {
                return Promise.reject(
                    new Error("The configured GazeMap WebSocket URL is invalid.")
                );
            }

            const current = {
                socket,
                promise: null,
                rejectConnect: null,
                opened: false,
                intentionalClose: false,
            };
            connection = current;

            current.promise = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
                let settled = false;
                const timeout = global.setTimeout(() => {
                    const error = new Error(
                        "Timed out connecting to GazeMap. Check that the service is running and BRAINGUARD_ORIGINS allows this website."
                    );
                    if (current.rejectConnect) current.rejectConnect(error);
                    socket.close();
                }, timeoutMs);

                const settle = (callback, value) => {
                    if (settled) return false;
                    settled = true;
                    global.clearTimeout(timeout);
                    current.rejectConnect = null;
                    callback(value);
                    return true;
                };
                current.rejectConnect = error => settle(reject, error);

                socket.addEventListener("open", () => {
                    current.opened = true;
                    settle(resolve);
                }, { once: true });

                socket.addEventListener("error", () => {
                    if (current.opened) {
                        rejectPendingRequests(new Error(
                            "GazeMap encountered a connection error."
                        ));
                        socket.close();
                        return;
                    }

                    current.rejectConnect(new Error(
                        "Could not connect to GazeMap. Check that the service is running and BRAINGUARD_ORIGINS allows this website."
                    ));
                    socket.close();
                }, { once: true });

                socket.addEventListener("message", event => {
                    let message;
                    try {
                        message = JSON.parse(event.data);
                    } catch {
                        rejectPendingRequests(new Error(
                            "GazeMap returned an unreadable response."
                        ));
                        return;
                    }

                    const requestId = String(message.requestId);
                    const pending = pendingRequests.get(requestId);
                    if (!pending) return;

                    pendingRequests.delete(requestId);
                    global.clearTimeout(pending.timeout);
                    if (message.type === "error" || message.error) {
                        pending.reject(new Error(
                            message.message || message.error || "GazeMap request failed."
                        ));
                    } else if (
                        pending.expectedType
                        && message.type !== pending.expectedType
                    ) {
                        pending.reject(new Error("Unexpected response from GazeMap."));
                    } else {
                        pending.resolve(message);
                    }
                });

                socket.addEventListener("close", event => {
                    const error = new Error(
                        event.reason || "GazeMap connection was closed."
                    );
                    if (!current.opened && current.rejectConnect) {
                        current.rejectConnect(error);
                    }
                    rejectPendingRequests(error);
                    if (connection === current) connection = null;
                    if (
                        current.opened
                        && !current.intentionalClose
                        && options.onClose
                    ) {
                        options.onClose(error);
                    }
                });
            });

            return current.promise;
        }

        function request(type, payload = {}, expectedType = type, timeoutMs = 20000) {
            if (!connection || connection.socket.readyState !== global.WebSocket.OPEN) {
                return Promise.reject(
                    new Error("GazeMap connection is not available.")
                );
            }

            const requestId = String(++requestCounter);
            return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
                const timeout = global.setTimeout(() => {
                    pendingRequests.delete(requestId);
                    reject(new Error("GazeMap did not respond in time."));
                }, timeoutMs);
                pendingRequests.set(requestId, {
                    expectedType,
                    resolve,
                    reject,
                    timeout,
                });
                try {
                    connection.socket.send(JSON.stringify({
                        ...payload,
                        type,
                        requestId,
                    }));
                } catch (error) {
                    global.clearTimeout(timeout);
                    pendingRequests.delete(requestId);
                    reject(error);
                }
            });
        }

        function close() {
            const current = connection;
            if (!current) return;

            current.intentionalClose = true;
            connection = null;
            const error = new Error("The browser camera session has ended.");
            if (!current.opened) current.rejectConnect(error);
            rejectPendingRequests(error);
            if (
                current.socket.readyState === global.WebSocket.CONNECTING
                || current.socket.readyState === global.WebSocket.OPEN
            ) {
                current.socket.close(1000, "Session ended.");
            }
        }

        return { connect, request, close };
    }

    global.GazeSocket = { create: createGazeSocket };
})(window);

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
    const runner = document.getElementById("memoryTrackingTest");
    if (!runner) return;

    const assessmentId = runner.dataset.assessmentId;
    const websocketUrl = runner.dataset.gazeWsUrl;
    const stage = document.getElementById("memoryStage");
    const canvas = document.getElementById("memoryCanvas");
    const context = canvas.getContext("2d");
    const calibrationUI = window.EyeCalibrationUI.create(stage);
    const video = document.getElementById("memoryVideo");
    const stageMessage = document.getElementById("memoryStageMessage");
    const stimulus = document.getElementById("memoryStimulus");
    const phaseLabel = document.getElementById("memoryPhaseLabel");
    const wordElement = document.getElementById("memoryWord");
    const instruction = document.getElementById("memoryInstruction");
    const responseButtons = document.getElementById("memoryResponses");
    const seenButton = document.getElementById("memorySeenButton");
    const notSeenButton = document.getElementById("memoryNotSeenButton");
    const startButton = document.getElementById("startMemoryTest");
    const cancelButton = document.getElementById("cancelMemoryTest");
    const statusText = document.getElementById("memoryStatus");
    const cameraStatus = document.getElementById("memoryCameraStatus");
    const faceStatus = document.getElementById("memoryFaceStatus");
    const progressTrack = document.querySelector(".eye-progress-track");
    const progressFill = document.getElementById("memoryProgress");
    const progressText = document.getElementById("memoryProgressText");

    const calibrationTargets = calibrationUI.targets;
    const encodingDurationMs = 3000;
    const retentionDurationMs = 5000;
    const recognitionTimeoutMs = 5000;
    const frameIntervalMs = 90;
    const captureCanvas = document.createElement("canvas");
    let captureContext = captureCanvas.getContext("2d");

    let active = false;
    let stream = null;
    let sessionId = null;
    let samples = [];
    let trials = [];
    let currentStimulus = null;
    let currentPhase = null;
    let currentImageId = null;
    let currentResponse = null;
    let currentResponseAt = 0;
    let currentTrialStartedAt = 0;
    let calibrationPointCount = 0;
    let mappingMethod = null;
    let screenInitialized = false;
    let canvasWidth = 0;
    let canvasHeight = 0;
    let pixelRatio = 1;
    let testStartedAt = 0;
    let completing = false;
    const gazeSocket = window.GazeSocket.create(websocketUrl, {
        onClose: error => {
            if (active) interruptTest(error.message);
        },
    });

    function setProgress(value) {
        const progress = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Number(value) || 0));
        progressFill.style.width = `${progress}%`;
        progressText.textContent = `${Math.round(progress)}%`;
        progressTrack.setAttribute("aria-valuenow", String(Math.round(progress)));
    }

    function wait(milliseconds) {
        return new Promise(resolve => window.setTimeout(resolve, milliseconds));
    }

    function ensureActive() {
        if (!active) throw new Error("The assessment was interrupted.");
    }

    function resizeCanvas() {
        const bounds = stage.getBoundingClientRect();
        const previousWidth = canvasWidth;
        const previousHeight = canvasHeight;
        pixelRatio = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
        canvasWidth = Math.max(1, Math.round(bounds.width));
        canvasHeight = Math.max(1, Math.round(bounds.height));
        canvas.width = Math.round(canvasWidth * pixelRatio);
        canvas.height = Math.round(canvasHeight * pixelRatio);
        context.setTransform(pixelRatio, 0, 0, pixelRatio, 0, 0);

        if (
            active
            && screenInitialized
            && (previousWidth !== canvasWidth || previousHeight !== canvasHeight)
        ) {
            interruptTest("Screen size changed. Restart the test to recalibrate.");
            return;
        }
        drawStage();
    }

    function drawStage() {
        context.clearRect(0, 0, canvasWidth, canvasHeight);
        if (!currentStimulus) return;

        const [fractionX, fractionY] = currentStimulus.position;
        const x = fractionX * canvasWidth;
        const y = fractionY * canvasHeight;
        const radius = Math.max(10, Math.min(19, canvasWidth / 55));
        context.beginPath();
        context.arc(x, y, radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        context.fillStyle = "#fbbf24";
        context.fill();
        context.strokeStyle = "#ffffff";
        context.lineWidth = 2;
        context.stroke();
        context.beginPath();
        context.arc(x, y, 3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        context.fillStyle = "#0f172a";
        context.fill();
    }

    async function requestJson(url, options = {}) {
        const response = await fetch(url, {
            ...options,
            headers: {
                "Content-Type": "application/json",
                ...options.headers,
            },
        });
        const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
        if (!response.ok || data.success === false) {
            throw new Error(data.message || `Request failed (${response.status}).`);
        }
        return data;
    }

    function requestSocket(type, payload = {}, expectedType) {
        return gazeSocket.request(type, payload, expectedType);
    }

    function connectSocket() {
        return gazeSocket.connect();
    }

    function stopCamera() {
        calibrationUI.hide();
        gazeSocket.close();
        if (stream) {
            stream.getTracks().forEach(track => track.stop());
            stream = null;
        }
        video.srcObject = null;
        stage.classList.remove("eye-camera-active");
        cameraStatus.textContent = "Camera is off.";
    }

    async function exitFullscreen() {
        if (document.fullscreenElement === runner) {
            try {
                await document.exitFullscreen();
            } catch (error) {
                console.error("Could not exit memory assessment fullscreen:", error);
            }
        }
    }

    async function cancelServerSession(keepalive = false) {
        if (!sessionId) return;
        const cancellingSessionId = sessionId;
        sessionId = null;
        try {
            await requestJson(
                `/eye-assessment/${encodeURIComponent(assessmentId)}/memory-tracking/browser/${encodeURIComponent(cancellingSessionId)}/cancel`,
                { method: "POST", keepalive }
            );
        } catch (error) {
            console.error("Could not cancel the memory session:", error);
        }
    }

    function clearStimulus() {
        currentStimulus = null;
        currentPhase = null;
        currentImageId = null;
        stimulus.hidden = true;
        responseButtons.hidden = true;
        stageMessage.hidden = false;
        drawStage();
    }

    function interruptTest(message) {
        if (!active) return;
        active = false;
        stopCamera();
        clearStimulus();
        stage.classList.remove("eye-test-active");
        startButton.disabled = false;
        cancelButton.hidden = true;
        stageMessage.textContent = message;
        statusText.textContent = message;
        void cancelServerSession();
        void exitFullscreen();
    }

    function encodeCaptureFrame() {
        return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
            captureCanvas.toBlob(blob => {
                if (!blob) {
                    reject(new Error("Could not encode the browser camera frame."));
                    return;
                }
                const reader = new FileReader();
                reader.addEventListener("load", () => {
                    if (typeof reader.result !== "string") {
                        reject(new Error("Could not encode the browser camera frame."));
                        return;
                    }
                    resolve(reader.result);
                }, { once: true });
                reader.addEventListener("error", () => {
                    reject(new Error("Could not read the encoded camera frame."));
                }, { once: true });
                reader.readAsDataURL(blob);
            }, "image/jpeg", 0.68);
        });
    }

    async function captureFrame(returnRaw = false) {
        if (video.readyState < HTMLMediaElement.HAVE_CURRENT_DATA) {
            throw new Error("Waiting for the camera image.");
        }
        const scale = Math.min(1, 640 / video.videoWidth);
        const captureWidth = Math.max(1, Math.round(video.videoWidth * scale));
        const captureHeight = Math.max(1, Math.round(video.videoHeight * scale));
        if (
            captureCanvas.width !== captureWidth
            || captureCanvas.height !== captureHeight
        ) {
            captureCanvas.width = captureWidth;
            captureCanvas.height = captureHeight;
            captureContext = captureCanvas.getContext("2d");
        }
        captureContext.drawImage(video, 0, 0, captureWidth, captureHeight);
        const capturedAt = performance.now();
        const frame = await requestSocket(
            "frame",
            {
                imageBase64: await encodeCaptureFrame(),
                timestampMs: Math.round(capturedAt),
                returnRaw,
            },
            "frame"
        );
        if (frame.error) throw new Error(frame.error);
        return { ...frame, capturedAt };
    }

    async function initializeGazeMap() {
        await connectSocket();
        await requestSocket(
            "init",
            { widthPx: canvasWidth, heightPx: canvasHeight, widthMm: 345 },
            "initialized"
        );
        screenInitialized = true;
    }

    async function calibrate() {
        const calibrationPoints = [];
        calibrationUI.begin();
        stageMessage.hidden = true;
        for (let index = 0; index < calibrationTargets.length && active; index += 1) {
            const position = calibrationTargets[index];
            calibrationUI.showTarget(index + 1, position);
            currentStimulus = { phase: "calibration", position };
            statusText.textContent =
                `Calibration ${index + 1} of ${calibrationTargets.length}: look at the dot.`;
            drawStage();
            await wait(600);

            const features = [];
            for (let frameIndex = 0; frameIndex < 5 && active; frameIndex += 1) {
                const frameStartedAt = performance.now();
                const frame = await captureFrame(true);
                if (frame.valid && Array.isArray(frame.features)) {
                    features.push(frame.features);
                }
                await wait(Math.max(0, frameIntervalMs - (performance.now() - frameStartedAt)));
            }
            calibrationPoints.push({
                targetX: position[0],
                targetY: position[1],
                features,
            });
            calibrationUI.completeTarget(index + 1);
            setProgress(((index + 1) / calibrationTargets.length) * 20);
        }
        ensureActive();

        calibrationUI.fitting();
        currentStimulus = null;
        stageMessage.hidden = false;
        stageMessage.textContent = "Fitting gaze calibration...";
        drawStage();
        const calibration = await requestSocket(
            "calibrate",
            { points: calibrationPoints },
            "calibration"
        );
        if (!calibration.success) {
            throw new Error(calibration.message || "Gaze calibration failed.");
        }
        calibrationPointCount = calibration.calibrationPointCount;
        mappingMethod = calibration.mappingMethod;
        calibrationUI.complete();
    }

    function showStimulus(label, word, instructionText, showResponses = false) {
        stageMessage.hidden = true;
        stimulus.hidden = false;
        phaseLabel.textContent = label;
        wordElement.textContent = word;
        instruction.textContent = instructionText;
        responseButtons.hidden = !showResponses;
        if (showResponses) {
            seenButton.disabled = false;
            notSeenButton.disabled = false;
        }
    }

    function shuffle(items) {
        const shuffled = [...items];
        for (let index = shuffled.length - 1; index > 0; index -= 1) {
            const swapIndex = Math.floor(Math.random() * (index + 1));
            [shuffled[index], shuffled[swapIndex]] =
                [shuffled[swapIndex], shuffled[index]];
        }
        return shuffled;
    }

    function submitResponse(response) {
        if (!active || currentPhase !== "recognition" || currentResponse !== null) return;
        currentResponse = response;
        currentResponseAt = performance.now();
        seenButton.disabled = true;
        notSeenButton.disabled = true;
    }

    function handleKeydown(event) {
        if (event.repeat || currentPhase !== "recognition") return;
        const key = event.key.toLowerCase();
        if (key === "y") submitResponse("seen");
        else if (key === "n") submitResponse("not_seen");
    }

    function createGazeSample(frame, phase, imageId) {
        const valid = frame.valid
            && Number.isFinite(frame.xFraction)
            && Number.isFinite(frame.yFraction)
            && frame.xFraction >= 0
            && frame.xFraction <= 1
            && frame.yFraction >= 0
            && frame.yFraction <= 1;
        const sample = {
            timeSeconds: Math.max(0, (frame.capturedAt - testStartedAt) / 1000),
            xPx: valid ? frame.xFraction * canvasWidth : null,
            yPx: valid ? frame.yFraction * canvasHeight : null,
            valid,
            phase,
            imageId: imageId || null,
        };
        samples.push(sample);
        if (frame.reason === "face_not_detected") {
            faceStatus.textContent = "Face not detected. Adjust your position and lighting.";
        } else if (valid && samples.length % 20 === 0) {
            faceStatus.textContent = `${samples.length} gaze samples recorded.`;
        }
        return sample;
    }

    async function captureMemorySample(phase, imageId) {
        const frameStartedAt = performance.now();
        const frame = await captureFrame();
        createGazeSample(frame, phase, imageId);
        const delay = Math.max(0, frameIntervalMs - (performance.now() - frameStartedAt));
        await wait(delay);
    }

    function trialFixations(phase, imageId, startedAt, endedAt) {
        const stageBounds = stage.getBoundingClientRect();
        const wordBounds = stimulus.getBoundingClientRect();
        const left = wordBounds.left - stageBounds.left;
        const top = wordBounds.top - stageBounds.top;
        const right = left + wordBounds.width;
        const bottom = top + wordBounds.height;
        const trialSamples = samples.filter(sample =>
            sample.phase === phase
            && sample.imageId === imageId
            && sample.timeSeconds >= startedAt
            && sample.timeSeconds <= endedAt
        );
        const fixationSamples = trialSamples.filter(sample =>
            sample.valid
            && sample.xPx >= left
            && sample.xPx <= right
            && sample.yPx >= top
            && sample.yPx <= bottom
        );
        const sampleIntervalMs = trialSamples.length > 1
            ? ((trialSamples[trialSamples.length - 1].timeSeconds
                - trialSamples[0].timeSeconds) * 1000) / (trialSamples.length - 1)
            : frameIntervalMs;
        const trialDurationMs = Math.max(0, (endedAt - startedAt) * 1000);
        return {
            fixationCount: fixationSamples.length,
            fixationDurationMs: Math.min(
                5000,
                trialDurationMs,
                fixationSamples.length * sampleIntervalMs
            ),
        };
    }

    async function observeFor(durationMs, phase, imageId, progressCallback) {
        const startedAt = performance.now();
        const endAt = startedAt + durationMs;
        while (active && performance.now() < endAt) {
            await captureMemorySample(phase, imageId);
            if (progressCallback) {
                progressCallback(Math.min(1, (performance.now() - startedAt) / durationMs));
            }
        }
        ensureActive();
        return {
            startedAt: (startedAt - testStartedAt) / 1000,
            endedAt: (performance.now() - testStartedAt) / 1000,
        };
    }

    async function runRecognitionTrial(word, isOld, index) {
        currentPhase = "recognition";
        currentImageId = word;
        currentResponse = null;
        currentResponseAt = 0;
        currentTrialStartedAt = performance.now();
        showStimulus(
            `Recognition ${index + 1} of 16`,
            word,
            "Have you seen this word before? Choose an answer within five seconds.",
            true
        );
        statusText.textContent = "Choose Seen or Not seen. You can also press Y or N.";
        const endAt = currentTrialStartedAt + recognitionTimeoutMs;
        while (active && currentResponse === null && performance.now() < endAt) {
            const remaining = endAt - performance.now();
            await captureMemorySample(
                "recognition",
                word
            );
            setProgress(50 + ((index + 1 - remaining / recognitionTimeoutMs) / 16) * 50);
        }
        ensureActive();

        const response = currentResponse;
        const responseTimeMs = response === null
            ? null
            : Math.min(
                recognitionTimeoutMs,
                currentResponseAt - currentTrialStartedAt
            );
        const trialStartedAtSeconds =
            (currentTrialStartedAt - testStartedAt) / 1000;
        const trialEndedAtSeconds = (performance.now() - testStartedAt) / 1000;
        const fixation = trialFixations(
            "recognition",
            word,
            trialStartedAtSeconds,
            trialEndedAtSeconds
        );
        trials.push({
            imageId: word,
            phase: "recognition",
            isOld,
            userResponse: response,
            isCorrect: response === null
                ? false
                : (response === "seen") === isOld,
            responseTimeMs,
            ...fixation,
        });
        currentPhase = null;
        currentImageId = null;
        responseButtons.hidden = true;
    }

    async function runMemoryTask() {
        samples = [];
        trials = [];
        testStartedAt = performance.now();
        const oldWords = shuffle([
            "APPLE", "BICYCLE", "CLOCK", "CHAIR", "CAR",
            "BOOK", "FLOWER", "CUP", "UMBRELLA", "CAMERA",
        ]).slice(0, 8);
        const newWords = shuffle([
            "TRAIN", "BRIDGE", "PIANO", "ORANGE", "RIVER",
            "MOUNTAIN", "PENCIL", "WINDOW", "PLANT", "TELEPHONE",
        ]).slice(0, 8);
        const recognitionWords = shuffle([
            ...oldWords.map(word => ({ word, isOld: true })),
            ...newWords.map(word => ({ word, isOld: false })),
        ]);

        for (let index = 0; index < oldWords.length; index += 1) {
            ensureActive();
            const word = oldWords[index];
            currentPhase = "encoding";
            currentImageId = word;
            showStimulus(
                `Study ${index + 1} of 8`,
                word,
                "Read this word and try to remember it."
            );
            statusText.textContent = "Study each word as it appears.";
            const interval = await observeFor(
                encodingDurationMs,
                "encoding",
                word,
                fraction => setProgress(20 + ((index + fraction) / 8) * 25)
            );
            const fixation = trialFixations(
                "encoding",
                word,
                interval.startedAt,
                interval.endedAt
            );
            trials.push({
                imageId: word,
                phase: "encoding",
                isOld: true,
                userResponse: null,
                isCorrect: null,
                responseTimeMs: null,
                ...fixation,
            });
            await wait(120);
        }

        ensureActive();
        currentPhase = "retention";
        currentImageId = null;
        showStimulus(
            "Retention interval",
            "Take a short pause",
            "Keep the words in mind. Recognition begins in a few seconds."
        );
        statusText.textContent = "Short retention interval.";
        await observeFor(
            retentionDurationMs,
            "retention",
            null,
            fraction => setProgress(45 + fraction * 5)
        );

        for (let index = 0; index < recognitionWords.length; index += 1) {
            ensureActive();
            const trial = recognitionWords[index];
            await runRecognitionTrial(trial.word, trial.isOld, index);
            setProgress(50 + ((index + 1) / recognitionWords.length) * 50);
            await wait(120);
        }

        ensureActive();
        await completeAssessment();
    }

    async function completeAssessment() {
        completing = true;
        active = false;
        stopCamera();
        stage.classList.remove("eye-test-active");
        cancelButton.hidden = true;
        clearStimulus();
        statusText.textContent = "Analyzing gaze and recognition results...";
        stageMessage.textContent = "Preparing your memory tracking results...";
        stageMessage.hidden = false;

        const result = await requestJson(
            `/eye-assessment/${encodeURIComponent(assessmentId)}/memory-tracking/browser/${encodeURIComponent(sessionId)}/complete`,
            {
                method: "POST",
                body: JSON.stringify({
                    samples,
                    trials,
                    widthPx: canvasWidth,
                    heightPx: canvasHeight,
                    calibrationPointCount,
                    mappingMethod,
                }),
            }
        );
        sessionId = null;
        setProgress(100);
        statusText.textContent = "Assessment saved. Opening your results...";
        await exitFullscreen();
        window.location.assign(result.resultUrl);
    }

    async function startTest() {
        if (active) return;
        if (!navigator.mediaDevices || !navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia) {
            statusText.textContent =
                "Camera access is unavailable. Use a secure connection or localhost.";
            return;
        }

        active = true;
        startButton.disabled = true;
        cancelButton.hidden = false;
        screenInitialized = false;
        sessionId = null;
        samples = [];
        trials = [];
        calibrationPointCount = 0;
        mappingMethod = null;
        completing = false;
        setProgress(0);
        statusText.textContent = "Entering fullscreen...";

        try {
            if (!runner.requestFullscreen) {
                throw new Error("Fullscreen mode is not supported by this browser.");
            }
            await runner.requestFullscreen();
            ensureActive();
            resizeCanvas();
            stage.classList.add("eye-test-active");
            stageMessage.hidden = false;
            stageMessage.textContent = "Starting the browser camera...";
            statusText.textContent = "Requesting camera permission...";

            const permissionReminder = window.setTimeout(() => {
                if (active) {
                    statusText.textContent =
                        "Still waiting for camera permission. Allow access in the browser prompt or site settings.";
                }
            }, 10000);
            let cameraStream;
            try {
                cameraStream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
                    video: {
                        width: { ideal: 640 },
                        height: { ideal: 480 },
                        facingMode: "user",
                    },
                    audio: false,
                });
            } finally {
                window.clearTimeout(permissionReminder);
            }
            if (!active) {
                cameraStream.getTracks().forEach(track => track.stop());
                return;
            }
            statusText.textContent = "Camera permission granted. Starting camera preview...";
            stream = cameraStream;
            stream.getVideoTracks().forEach(track => {
                track.addEventListener("ended", () => {
                    interruptTest("Camera disconnected. Restart the test and grant camera access.");
                }, { once: true });
            });
            video.srcObject = stream;
            await video.play();
            ensureActive();
            stage.classList.add("eye-camera-active");
            statusText.textContent = "Camera ready. Starting assessment session...";
            cameraStatus.textContent = "Browser camera is live.";
            faceStatus.textContent = "Connecting to GazeMap...";

            const started = await requestJson(
                `/eye-assessment/${encodeURIComponent(assessmentId)}/memory-tracking/browser/start`,
                { method: "POST" }
            );
            sessionId = started.sessionId;
            ensureActive();
            statusText.textContent = "Assessment session ready. Connecting to GazeMap...";
            await initializeGazeMap();
            ensureActive();
            statusText.textContent = "Calibration: look at each dot as it appears.";
            await calibrate();
            ensureActive();
            currentStimulus = null;
            stageMessage.textContent = "Calibration complete. The memory sequence is starting.";
            drawStage();
            await wait(350);
            calibrationUI.hide();
            ensureActive();
            await runMemoryTask();
        } catch (error) {
            const wasCancelled = !active && !completing;
            active = false;
            stopCamera();
            clearStimulus();
            stage.classList.remove("eye-test-active");
            startButton.disabled = false;
            cancelButton.hidden = true;
            if (!wasCancelled) {
                completing = false;
                stageMessage.hidden = false;
                stageMessage.textContent =
                    error.message || "The assessment could not be completed.";
                statusText.textContent =
                    `Unable to complete the test: ${stageMessage.textContent}`;
            }
            if (sessionId) await cancelServerSession(wasCancelled);
            await exitFullscreen();
        }
    }

    async function cancelTest() {
        if (!active) return;
        active = false;
        stopCamera();
        clearStimulus();
        stage.classList.remove("eye-test-active");
        startButton.disabled = false;
        cancelButton.hidden = true;
        statusText.textContent = "Memory tracking cancelled.";
        stageMessage.hidden = false;
        stageMessage.textContent = "The test was cancelled. You can start again when ready.";
        await cancelServerSession();
        await exitFullscreen();
    }

    function handleResize() {
        resizeCanvas();
    }

    function handleFullscreenChange() {
        if (active && document.fullscreenElement !== runner) {
            interruptTest("Fullscreen was exited. Restart the test to continue.");
        }
    }

    startButton.addEventListener("click", startTest);
    cancelButton.addEventListener("click", cancelTest);
    seenButton.addEventListener("click", () => submitResponse("seen"));
    notSeenButton.addEventListener("click", () => submitResponse("not_seen"));
    window.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeydown);
    window.addEventListener("resize", handleResize);
    document.addEventListener("fullscreenchange", handleFullscreenChange);
    window.addEventListener("pagehide", () => {
        active = false;
        stopCamera();
        if (sessionId) void cancelServerSession(true);
    });
    resizeCanvas();
});

FILE: C:\Users\surendra purohit\Documents\clean\public\js\eye-tests\saccadic.js
