document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
    const runner = document.getElementById("saccadicTest");
    if (!runner) return;

    const assessmentId = runner.dataset.assessmentId;
    const websocketUrl = runner.dataset.gazeWsUrl;
    const stage = document.getElementById("saccadicStage");
    const canvas = document.getElementById("saccadicCanvas");
    const context = canvas.getContext("2d");
    const calibrationUI = window.EyeCalibrationUI.create(stage);
    const video = document.getElementById("saccadicVideo");
    const startButton = document.getElementById("startSaccadicTest");
    const cancelButton = document.getElementById("cancelSaccadicTest");
    const statusText = document.getElementById("saccadicStatus");
    const stageMessage = document.getElementById("saccadicStageMessage");
    const progressTrack = document.querySelector(".eye-progress-track");
    const progressFill = document.getElementById("saccadicProgress");
    const progressText = document.getElementById("saccadicProgressText");

    const calibrationTargets = calibrationUI.targets;
    const trialCount = 12;
    const fixationDurationMs = 450;
    const targetDurationMs = 850;
    const frameIntervalMs = 90;

    let active = false;
    let stream = null;
    let sessionId = null;
    let samples = [];
    let targets = [];
    let currentStimulus = null;
    let calibrationPointCount = 0;
    let mappingMethod = null;
    let screenInitialized = false;
    let canvasWidth = 0;
    let canvasHeight = 0;
    let pixelRatio = 1;
    let testStartedAt = 0;
    let captureCanvas = document.createElement("canvas");
    let captureContext = captureCanvas.getContext("2d");
    const gazeSocket = window.GazeSocket.create(websocketUrl, {
        onClose: error => {
            if (active) interruptTest(error.message);
        },
    });

    function setProgress(value) {
        const progress = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Number(value) || 0));
        progressFill.style.width = `${progress}%`;
        progressText.textContent = `${Math.round(progress)}%`;
        progressTrack.setAttribute("aria-valuenow", String(Math.round(progress)));
    }

    function resizeCanvas() {
        const bounds = stage.getBoundingClientRect();
        const previousWidth = canvasWidth;
        const previousHeight = canvasHeight;
        pixelRatio = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
        canvasWidth = Math.max(1, Math.round(bounds.width));
        canvasHeight = Math.max(1, Math.round(bounds.height));
        canvas.width = Math.round(canvasWidth * pixelRatio);
        canvas.height = Math.round(canvasHeight * pixelRatio);
        context.setTransform(pixelRatio, 0, 0, pixelRatio, 0, 0);

        if (
            active
            && screenInitialized
            && (previousWidth !== canvasWidth || previousHeight !== canvasHeight)
        ) {
            interruptTest("Screen size changed. Restart the test to recalibrate.");
            return;
        }
        drawStage();
    }

    function drawStage() {
        context.clearRect(0, 0, canvasWidth, canvasHeight);
        context.fillStyle = "#10233a";
        context.fillRect(0, 0, canvasWidth / 2, canvasHeight);
        context.fillStyle = "#3a1c2a";
        context.fillRect(canvasWidth / 2, 0, canvasWidth / 2, canvasHeight);

        if (!currentStimulus) return;
        const [fractionX, fractionY] = currentStimulus.position;
        const x = fractionX * canvasWidth;
        const y = fractionY * canvasHeight;
        const radius = Math.max(9, Math.min(18, canvasWidth / 55));

        context.beginPath();
        context.arc(x, y, radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        context.fillStyle = currentStimulus.phase === "calibration"
            ? "#fbbf24"
            : "#ffffff";
        context.fill();
        context.strokeStyle = "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9)";
        context.lineWidth = 2;
        context.stroke();
    }

    function wait(milliseconds) {
        return new Promise(resolve => window.setTimeout(resolve, milliseconds));
    }

    async function requestJson(url, options = {}) {
        const response = await fetch(url, {
            ...options,
            headers: {
                "Content-Type": "application/json",
                ...options.headers
            }
        });
        const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
        if (!response.ok || data.success === false) {
            throw new Error(data.message || `Request failed (${response.status}).`);
        }
        return data;
    }

    function requestSocket(type, payload = {}, expectedType) {
        return gazeSocket.request(type, payload, expectedType);
    }

    function connectSocket() {
        return gazeSocket.connect();
    }

    function stopCamera() {
        calibrationUI.hide();
        gazeSocket.close();
        if (stream) {
            stream.getTracks().forEach(track => track.stop());
            stream = null;
        }
        video.srcObject = null;
        stage.classList.remove("eye-camera-active");
    }

    async function exitFullscreen() {
        if (document.fullscreenElement === runner) {
            try {
                await document.exitFullscreen();
            } catch (error) {
                console.error("Could not exit assessment fullscreen:", error);
            }
        }
    }

    async function cancelServerSession(keepalive = false) {
        if (!sessionId) return;
        const cancellingSessionId = sessionId;
        sessionId = null;
        try {
            await requestJson(
                `/eye-assessment/${encodeURIComponent(assessmentId)}/saccadic/${encodeURIComponent(cancellingSessionId)}/cancel`,
                { method: "POST", keepalive }
            );
        } catch (error) {
            console.error("Could not cancel the saccadic session:", error);
        }
    }

    function interruptTest(message) {
        if (!active) return;
        active = false;
        stopCamera();
        currentStimulus = null;
        stage.classList.remove("eye-test-active");
        startButton.disabled = false;
        cancelButton.hidden = true;
        stageMessage.textContent = message;
        statusText.textContent = message;
        void cancelServerSession();
        void exitFullscreen();
        drawStage();
    }

    async function captureFrame(returnRaw = false) {
        if (video.readyState < HTMLMediaElement.HAVE_CURRENT_DATA) {
            throw new Error("Waiting for the camera image.");
        }

        const scale = Math.min(1, 640 / video.videoWidth);
        captureCanvas.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(video.videoWidth * scale));
        captureCanvas.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(video.videoHeight * scale));
        captureContext.drawImage(
            video,
            0,
            0,
            captureCanvas.width,
            captureCanvas.height
        );
        const capturedAt = performance.now();
        const frame = await requestSocket(
            "frame",
            {
                imageBase64: captureCanvas.toDataURL("image/jpeg", 0.68),
                timestampMs: Math.round(capturedAt),
                returnRaw
            },
            "frame"
        );
        return { ...frame, capturedAt };
    }

    async function initializeGazeMap() {
        await connectSocket();
        const initialized = await requestSocket(
            "init",
            { widthPx: canvasWidth, heightPx: canvasHeight, widthMm: 345 },
            "initialized"
        );
        if (!initialized) throw new Error("GazeMap could not initialize the screen.");
        screenInitialized = true;
    }

    async function calibrate() {
        const calibrationPoints = [];
        calibrationUI.begin();
        for (let index = 0; index < calibrationTargets.length && active; index += 1) {
            const position = calibrationTargets[index];
            calibrationUI.showTarget(index + 1, position);
            currentStimulus = { phase: "calibration", position };
            stageMessage.textContent =
                `Calibration ${index + 1} of ${calibrationTargets.length}: look at the dot.`;
            drawStage();
            await wait(600);

            const features = [];
            for (let frameIndex = 0; frameIndex < 5 && active; frameIndex += 1) {
                const frameStartedAt = performance.now();
                const frame = await captureFrame(true);
                if (frame.error) throw new Error(frame.error);
                if (frame.valid && Array.isArray(frame.features)) {
                    features.push(frame.features);
                }
                await wait(Math.max(0, frameIntervalMs - (performance.now() - frameStartedAt)));
            }
            calibrationPoints.push({
                targetX: position[0],
                targetY: position[1],
                features
            });
            calibrationUI.completeTarget(index + 1);
            setProgress(((index + 1) / calibrationTargets.length) * 25);
        }
        if (!active) throw new Error("Assessment was interrupted.");

        calibrationUI.fitting();
        stageMessage.textContent = "Fitting gaze calibration...";
        currentStimulus = null;
        drawStage();
        const calibration = await requestSocket(
            "calibrate",
            { points: calibrationPoints },
            "calibration"
        );
        if (!calibration.success) {
            throw new Error(calibration.message || "Gaze calibration failed.");
        }
        calibrationPointCount = calibration.calibrationPointCount;
        mappingMethod = calibration.mappingMethod;
        calibrationUI.complete();
        return calibration;
    }

    function createTargets() {
        const sides = Array(6).fill("left").concat(Array(6).fill("right"));
        for (let index = sides.length - 1; index > 0; index -= 1) {
            const swapIndex = Math.floor(Math.random() * (index + 1));
            [sides[index], sides[swapIndex]] = [sides[swapIndex], sides[index]];
        }
        return sides.map((side, index) => ({
            id: `trial-${index + 1}`,
            side,
            xPx: Math.round((side === "left" ? 0.2 : 0.8) * canvasWidth),
            yPx: Math.round(0.5 * canvasHeight),
            cueOnsetAtSeconds: null,
            appearedAtSeconds: null,
            disappearedAtSeconds: null
        }));
    }

    function relativeSeconds(time) {
        return Math.max(0, (time - testStartedAt) / 1000);
    }

    async function collectSamples(durationMs, target) {
        const endAt = performance.now() + durationMs;
        while (active && performance.now() < endAt) {
            const frameStartedAt = performance.now();
            const frame = await captureFrame();
            if (frame.error) throw new Error(frame.error);
            const xFraction = frame.xFraction;
            const yFraction = frame.yFraction;
            const valid = frame.valid
                && Number.isFinite(xFraction)
                && Number.isFinite(yFraction)
                && xFraction >= 0
                && xFraction <= 1
                && yFraction >= 0
                && yFraction <= 1;

            samples.push({
                timeSeconds: relativeSeconds(frame.capturedAt),
                xPx: valid ? xFraction * canvasWidth : null,
                yPx: valid ? yFraction * canvasHeight : null,
                targetXPx: target.xPx,
                targetYPx: target.yPx,
                valid,
                onScreen: valid,
                distanceMm: Number.isFinite(frame.distanceMm) ? frame.distanceMm : null,
                reason: valid ? null : (frame.reason || "invalid_gaze")
            });
            if (!valid && frame.reason === "face_not_detected") {
                statusText.textContent = "Face not detected. Keep your face visible to the camera.";
            } else if (valid) {
                statusText.textContent = `Recording gaze samples: ${samples.length}.`;
            }
            await wait(Math.max(0, frameIntervalMs - (performance.now() - frameStartedAt)));
        }
        if (!active) throw new Error("Assessment was interrupted.");
    }

    async function runTrials() {
        samples = [];
        targets = createTargets();
        testStartedAt = performance.now();
        stageMessage.textContent = "Look at the center mark, then quickly look at the side target.";

        for (let index = 0; index < targets.length && active; index += 1) {
            const target = targets[index];
            target.cueOnsetAtSeconds = relativeSeconds(performance.now());
            currentStimulus = { phase: "cue", position: [0.5, 0.5] };
            drawStage();
            await collectSamples(fixationDurationMs, target);

            target.appearedAtSeconds = relativeSeconds(performance.now());
            currentStimulus = {
                phase: "target",
                position: [target.xPx / canvasWidth, target.yPx / canvasHeight]
            };
            drawStage();
            await collectSamples(targetDurationMs, target);
            target.disappearedAtSeconds = relativeSeconds(performance.now());
            currentStimulus = null;
            drawStage();
            setProgress(25 + ((index + 1) / trialCount) * 75);
        }

        if (!active) throw new Error("Assessment was interrupted.");
        await saveResults();
    }

    async function saveResults() {
        active = false;
        currentStimulus = null;
        stopCamera();
        stage.classList.remove("eye-test-active");
        cancelButton.hidden = true;
        statusText.textContent = "Analyzing gaze samples and saving your results...";
        stageMessage.textContent = "Preparing your saccadic assessment results...";
        drawStage();

        const result = await requestJson(
            `/eye-assessment/${encodeURIComponent(assessmentId)}/saccadic/${encodeURIComponent(sessionId)}/complete`,
            {
                method: "POST",
                body: JSON.stringify({
                    samples,
                    targets,
                    widthPx: canvasWidth,
                    heightPx: canvasHeight,
                    calibrationPointCount,
                    mappingMethod
                })
            }
        );
        sessionId = null;
        setProgress(100);
        statusText.textContent = "Assessment saved. Opening your results...";
        await exitFullscreen();
        window.location.assign(result.resultUrl);
    }

    async function startTest() {
        if (active) return;
        if (!navigator.mediaDevices?.getUserMedia) {
            statusText.textContent =
                "Camera access is unavailable. Use a secure connection or localhost.";
            return;
        }

        active = true;
        startButton.disabled = true;
        cancelButton.hidden = false;
        screenInitialized = false;
        sessionId = null;
        samples = [];
        targets = [];
        setProgress(0);
        statusText.textContent = "Entering fullscreen...";

        try {
            if (!runner.requestFullscreen) {
                throw new Error("Fullscreen mode is not supported by this browser.");
            }
            await runner.requestFullscreen();
            if (!active) return;
            resizeCanvas();
            stage.classList.add("eye-test-active");
            stageMessage.textContent = "Starting the browser camera...";
            statusText.textContent = "Requesting camera permission...";

            const permissionReminder = window.setTimeout(() => {
                if (active) {
                    statusText.textContent =
                        "Still waiting for camera permission. Allow access in the browser prompt or site settings.";
                }
            }, 10000);
            let cameraStream;
            try {
                cameraStream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
                    video: {
                        width: { ideal: 640 },
                        height: { ideal: 480 },
                        facingMode: "user"
                    },
                    audio: false
                });
            } finally {
                window.clearTimeout(permissionReminder);
            }
            if (!active) {
                cameraStream.getTracks().forEach(track => track.stop());
                return;
            }
            statusText.textContent = "Camera permission granted. Starting camera preview...";
            stream = cameraStream;
            stream.getVideoTracks().forEach(track => {
                track.addEventListener("ended", () => {
                    interruptTest(
                        "Camera disconnected. Restart the test and grant camera access."
                    );
                }, { once: true });
            });
            video.srcObject = stream;
            await video.play();
            if (!active) return;
            stage.classList.add("eye-camera-active");
            statusText.textContent = "Camera ready. Starting assessment session...";

            const started = await requestJson(
                `/eye-assessment/${encodeURIComponent(assessmentId)}/saccadic/start`,
                { method: "POST" }
            );
            sessionId = started.sessionId;
            if (!active) {
                await cancelServerSession(true);
                return;
            }
            statusText.textContent = "Assessment session ready. Connecting to GazeMap...";
            await initializeGazeMap();
            if (!active) return;
            statusText.textContent = "Calibrating. Follow each on-screen dot.";
            await calibrate();
            if (!active) return;

            statusText.textContent = "Calibration complete. Beginning the trials...";
            await wait(350);
            calibrationUI.hide();
            await runTrials();
        } catch (error) {
            active = false;
            stopCamera();
            currentStimulus = null;
            stage.classList.remove("eye-test-active");
            startButton.disabled = false;
            cancelButton.hidden = true;
            stageMessage.textContent = error.message || "The assessment could not be completed.";
            statusText.textContent = `Unable to complete the test: ${stageMessage.textContent}`;
            drawStage();
            if (sessionId) await cancelServerSession();
            await exitFullscreen();
        }
    }

    async function cancelTest() {
        if (!active) return;
        active = false;
        cancelButton.disabled = true;
        statusText.textContent = "Cancelling the assessment...";
        stopCamera();
        currentStimulus = null;
        stage.classList.remove("eye-test-active");
        drawStage();
        await cancelServerSession();
        cancelButton.hidden = true;
        cancelButton.disabled = false;
        startButton.disabled = false;
        statusText.textContent = "Assessment cancelled. Select Start Test to try again.";
        stageMessage.textContent = "Assessment cancelled.";
        await exitFullscreen();
    }

    startButton.addEventListener("click", startTest);
    cancelButton.addEventListener("click", cancelTest);

    document.addEventListener("fullscreenchange", () => {
        if (active && document.fullscreenElement !== runner) {
            interruptTest("Fullscreen was exited. Restart the assessment to continue.");
        } else if (document.fullscreenElement === runner) {
            resizeCanvas();
        }
    });

    window.addEventListener("resize", resizeCanvas);
    window.addEventListener("pagehide", () => {
        active = false;
        stopCamera();
        if (sessionId) void cancelServerSession(true);
    });
    resizeCanvas();
});

FILE: C:\Users\surendra purohit\Documents\clean\public\js\eye-tests\smooth-pursuit.js

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
    const runner = document.getElementById("smoothPursuitTest");
    if (!runner) return;

    const assessmentId = runner.dataset.assessmentId;
    const websocketUrl = runner.dataset.gazeWsUrl;
    const stage = document.getElementById("smoothPursuitStage");
    const canvas = document.getElementById("smoothPursuitCanvas");
    const context = canvas.getContext("2d");
    const calibrationUI = window.EyeCalibrationUI.create(stage);
    const video = document.getElementById("smoothPursuitVideo");
    const startButton = document.getElementById("startSmoothPursuitTest");
    const cancelButton = document.getElementById("cancelSmoothPursuitTest");
    const statusText = document.getElementById("smoothPursuitStatus");
    const stageMessage = document.getElementById("smoothPursuitStageMessage");
    const progressTrack = document.querySelector(".eye-progress-track");
    const progressFill = document.getElementById("smoothPursuitProgress");
    const progressText = document.getElementById("smoothPursuitProgressText");

    const calibrationTargets = calibrationUI.targets;
    const testDurationMs = 30000;
    const frameIntervalMs = 90;
    const pursuitCycles = 5;
    const pursuitAmplitudeFraction = 0.35;

    let active = false;
    let stream = null;
    let sessionId = null;
    let samples = [];
    let currentStimulus = null;
    let calibrationPointCount = 0;
    let mappingMethod = null;
    let screenInitialized = false;
    let canvasWidth = 0;
    let canvasHeight = 0;
    let pixelRatio = 1;
    let testStartedAt = 0;
    let animationFrame = null;
    let lastDrawnDot = null;
    let lastProgressValue = null;
    let captureCanvas = document.createElement("canvas");
    let captureContext = captureCanvas.getContext("2d");
    const gazeSocket = window.GazeSocket.create(websocketUrl, {
        onClose: error => {
            if (active) interruptTest(error.message);
        },
    });

    function setProgress(value) {
        const progress = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Number(value) || 0));
        const roundedProgress = Math.round(progress);
        if (roundedProgress === lastProgressValue) return;
        lastProgressValue = roundedProgress;
        progressFill.style.width = `${progress}%`;
        progressText.textContent = `${roundedProgress}%`;
        progressTrack.setAttribute("aria-valuenow", String(roundedProgress));
    }

    function resizeCanvas() {
        const bounds = stage.getBoundingClientRect();
        const previousWidth = canvasWidth;
        const previousHeight = canvasHeight;
        pixelRatio = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
        canvasWidth = Math.max(1, Math.round(bounds.width));
        canvasHeight = Math.max(1, Math.round(bounds.height));
        canvas.width = Math.round(canvasWidth * pixelRatio);
        canvas.height = Math.round(canvasHeight * pixelRatio);
        context.setTransform(pixelRatio, 0, 0, pixelRatio, 0, 0);
        lastDrawnDot = null;

        if (
            active
            && screenInitialized
            && (previousWidth !== canvasWidth || previousHeight !== canvasHeight)
        ) {
            interruptTest("Screen size changed. Restart the test to recalibrate.");
            return;
        }
        drawStage();
    }

    function drawStage() {
        context.clearRect(0, 0, canvasWidth, canvasHeight);
        lastDrawnDot = null;
        if (currentStimulus) {
            drawDot(currentStimulus.position, currentStimulus.phase);
        }
    }

    function drawDot(position, phase) {
        const x = position[0] * canvasWidth;
        const y = position[1] * canvasHeight;
        const radius = Math.max(9, Math.min(18, canvasWidth / 55));
        if (lastDrawnDot) {
            const padding = 4;
            context.clearRect(
                lastDrawnDot.x - lastDrawnDot.radius - padding,
                lastDrawnDot.y - lastDrawnDot.radius - padding,
                (lastDrawnDot.radius + padding) * 2,
                (lastDrawnDot.radius + padding) * 2
            );
        }
        context.beginPath();
        context.arc(x, y, radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        context.fillStyle = phase === "calibration"
            ? "#fbbf24"
            : "#ffffff";
        context.fill();
        if (phase === "pursuit") {
            context.beginPath();
            context.arc(x, y, Math.max(3, radius / 4), 0, Math.PI * 2);
            context.fillStyle = "#ef4444";
            context.fill();
        }
        lastDrawnDot = { x, y, radius };
    }

    function wait(milliseconds) {
        return new Promise(resolve => window.setTimeout(resolve, milliseconds));
    }

    function encodeCaptureFrame() {
        return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
            captureCanvas.toBlob(blob => {
                if (!blob) {
                    reject(new Error("Could not encode the browser camera frame."));
                    return;
                }
                const reader = new FileReader();
                reader.addEventListener("load", () => {
                    if (typeof reader.result !== "string") {
                        reject(new Error("Could not encode the browser camera frame."));
                        return;
                    }
                    resolve(reader.result);
                }, { once: true });
                reader.addEventListener("error", () => {
                    reject(new Error("Could not read the encoded camera frame."));
                }, { once: true });
                reader.readAsDataURL(blob);
            }, "image/jpeg", 0.68);
        });
    }

    async function requestJson(url, options = {}) {
        const response = await fetch(url, {
            ...options,
            headers: {
                "Content-Type": "application/json",
                ...options.headers
            }
        });
        const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
        if (!response.ok || data.success === false) {
            throw new Error(data.message || `Request failed (${response.status}).`);
        }
        return data;
    }

    function requestSocket(type, payload = {}, expectedType) {
        return gazeSocket.request(type, payload, expectedType);
    }

    function connectSocket() {
        return gazeSocket.connect();
    }

    function stopCamera() {
        calibrationUI.hide();
        if (animationFrame !== null) {
            window.cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrame);
            animationFrame = null;
        }
        gazeSocket.close();
        if (stream) {
            stream.getTracks().forEach(track => track.stop());
            stream = null;
        }
        video.srcObject = null;
        stage.classList.remove("eye-camera-active");
    }

    async function exitFullscreen() {
        if (document.fullscreenElement === runner) {
            try {
                await document.exitFullscreen();
            } catch (error) {
                console.error("Could not exit assessment fullscreen:", error);
            }
        }
    }

    async function cancelServerSession(keepalive = false) {
        if (!sessionId) return;
        const cancellingSessionId = sessionId;
        sessionId = null;
        try {
            await requestJson(
                `/eye-assessment/${encodeURIComponent(assessmentId)}/smooth-pursuit/browser/${encodeURIComponent(cancellingSessionId)}/cancel`,
                { method: "POST", keepalive }
            );
        } catch (error) {
            console.error("Could not cancel the smooth pursuit session:", error);
        }
    }

    function interruptTest(message) {
        if (!active) return;
        active = false;
        stopCamera();
        currentStimulus = null;
        stage.classList.remove("eye-test-active");
        startButton.disabled = false;
        cancelButton.hidden = true;
        stageMessage.textContent = message;
        statusText.textContent = message;
        void cancelServerSession();
        void exitFullscreen();
        drawStage();
    }

    async function captureFrame(returnRaw = false) {
        if (video.readyState < HTMLMediaElement.HAVE_CURRENT_DATA) {
            throw new Error("Waiting for the camera image.");
        }
        const scale = Math.min(1, 640 / video.videoWidth);
        const captureWidth = Math.max(1, Math.round(video.videoWidth * scale));
        const captureHeight = Math.max(1, Math.round(video.videoHeight * scale));
        if (
            captureCanvas.width !== captureWidth
            || captureCanvas.height !== captureHeight
        ) {
            captureCanvas.width = captureWidth;
            captureCanvas.height = captureHeight;
            captureContext = captureCanvas.getContext("2d");
        }
        captureContext.drawImage(
            video,
            0,
            0,
            captureCanvas.width,
            captureCanvas.height
        );
        const capturedAt = performance.now();
        const imageBase64 = await encodeCaptureFrame();
        const frame = await requestSocket(
            "frame",
            {
                imageBase64,
                timestampMs: Math.round(capturedAt),
                returnRaw
            },
            "frame"
        );
        return { ...frame, capturedAt };
    }

    async function initializeGazeMap() {
        await connectSocket();
        await requestSocket(
            "init",
            { widthPx: canvasWidth, heightPx: canvasHeight, widthMm: 345 },
            "initialized"
        );
        screenInitialized = true;
    }

    async function calibrate() {
        const calibrationPoints = [];
        calibrationUI.begin();
        for (let index = 0; index < calibrationTargets.length && active; index += 1) {
            const position = calibrationTargets[index];
            calibrationUI.showTarget(index + 1, position);
            currentStimulus = { phase: "calibration", position };
            stageMessage.textContent =
                `Calibration ${index + 1} of ${calibrationTargets.length}: look at the dot.`;
            drawStage();
            await wait(600);

            const features = [];
            for (let frameIndex = 0; frameIndex < 5 && active; frameIndex += 1) {
                const frameStartedAt = performance.now();
                const frame = await captureFrame(true);
                if (frame.error) throw new Error(frame.error);
                if (frame.valid && Array.isArray(frame.features)) {
                    features.push(frame.features);
                }
                await wait(Math.max(0, frameIntervalMs - (performance.now() - frameStartedAt)));
            }
            calibrationPoints.push({
                targetX: position[0],
                targetY: position[1],
                features
            });
            calibrationUI.completeTarget(index + 1);
            setProgress(((index + 1) / calibrationTargets.length) * 25);
        }
        if (!active) throw new Error("Assessment was interrupted.");

        calibrationUI.fitting();
        currentStimulus = null;
        stageMessage.textContent = "Fitting gaze calibration...";
        drawStage();
        const calibration = await requestSocket(
            "calibrate",
            { points: calibrationPoints },
            "calibration"
        );
        if (!calibration.success) {
            throw new Error(calibration.message || "Gaze calibration failed.");
        }
        calibrationPointCount = calibration.calibrationPointCount;
        mappingMethod = calibration.mappingMethod;
        calibrationUI.complete();
    }

    function pursuitPosition(elapsedSeconds) {
        const phase = 2 * Math.PI
            * pursuitCycles
            * elapsedSeconds
            / (testDurationMs / 1000);
        return [
            0.5 - pursuitAmplitudeFraction * Math.cos(phase),
            0.5
        ];
    }

    function drawPursuit(timestamp) {
        if (!active) return;
        const elapsedSeconds = Math.max(0, (timestamp - testStartedAt) / 1000);
        currentStimulus = {
            phase: "pursuit",
            position: pursuitPosition(elapsedSeconds)
        };
        drawDot(currentStimulus.position, currentStimulus.phase);
        setProgress(25 + (elapsedSeconds / (testDurationMs / 1000)) * 75);
        if (elapsedSeconds < testDurationMs / 1000) {
            animationFrame = window.requestAnimationFrame(drawPursuit);
        } else {
            animationFrame = null;
        }
    }

    async function runTracking() {
        samples = [];
        testStartedAt = performance.now();
        stageMessage.textContent = "Follow the moving dot smoothly with your eyes.";
        currentStimulus = {
            phase: "pursuit",
            position: pursuitPosition(0)
        };
        drawStage();
        animationFrame = window.requestAnimationFrame(drawPursuit);
        const endAt = testStartedAt + testDurationMs;

        while (active && performance.now() < endAt) {
            const frameStartedAt = performance.now();
            const frame = await captureFrame();
            if (frame.error) throw new Error(frame.error);
            const elapsedSeconds = (frame.capturedAt - testStartedAt) / 1000;
            const targetPosition = pursuitPosition(
                Math.min(elapsedSeconds, testDurationMs / 1000)
            );
            const valid = frame.valid
                && Number.isFinite(frame.xFraction)
                && Number.isFinite(frame.yFraction)
                && frame.xFraction >= 0
                && frame.xFraction <= 1
                && frame.yFraction >= 0
                && frame.yFraction <= 1;

            samples.push({
                timeSeconds: elapsedSeconds,
                xPx: valid ? frame.xFraction * canvasWidth : null,
                yPx: valid ? frame.yFraction * canvasHeight : null,
                targetXPx: targetPosition[0] * canvasWidth,
                targetYPx: targetPosition[1] * canvasHeight,
                valid,
                onScreen: valid,
                distanceMm: Number.isFinite(frame.distanceMm) ? frame.distanceMm : null,
                reason: valid ? null : (frame.reason || "invalid_gaze")
            });
            if (!valid && frame.reason === "face_not_detected") {
                statusText.textContent =
                    "Face not detected. Keep your face visible to the camera.";
            } else if (valid && samples.length % 10 === 0) {
                statusText.textContent =
                    `Tracking gaze: ${samples.length} samples recorded.`;
            }
            await wait(Math.max(0, frameIntervalMs - (performance.now() - frameStartedAt)));
        }
        if (!active) throw new Error("Assessment was interrupted.");
        if (animationFrame !== null) {
            window.cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrame);
            animationFrame = null;
        }
        currentStimulus = null;
        drawStage();
        await saveResults();
    }

    async function saveResults() {
        active = false;
        stopCamera();
        stage.classList.remove("eye-test-active");
        cancelButton.hidden = true;
        statusText.textContent = "Analyzing gaze samples and saving your results...";
        stageMessage.textContent = "Preparing your smooth pursuit results...";
        drawStage();

        const result = await requestJson(
            `/eye-assessment/${encodeURIComponent(assessmentId)}/smooth-pursuit/browser/${encodeURIComponent(sessionId)}/complete`,
            {
                method: "POST",
                body: JSON.stringify({
                    samples,
                    widthPx: canvasWidth,
                    heightPx: canvasHeight,
                    calibrationPointCount,
                    mappingMethod
                })
            }
        );
        sessionId = null;
        setProgress(100);
        statusText.textContent = "Assessment saved. Opening your results...";
        await exitFullscreen();
        window.location.assign(result.resultUrl);
    }

    async function startTest() {
        if (active) return;
        if (!navigator.mediaDevices?.getUserMedia) {
            statusText.textContent =
                "Camera access is unavailable. Use a secure connection or localhost.";
            return;
        }

        active = true;
        startButton.disabled = true;
        cancelButton.hidden = false;
        screenInitialized = false;
        sessionId = null;
        samples = [];
        setProgress(0);
        statusText.textContent = "Entering fullscreen...";

        try {
            if (!runner.requestFullscreen) {
                throw new Error("Fullscreen mode is not supported by this browser.");
            }
            await runner.requestFullscreen();
            if (!active) return;
            resizeCanvas();
            stage.classList.add("eye-test-active");
            stageMessage.textContent = "Starting the browser camera...";
            statusText.textContent = "Requesting camera permission...";

            const permissionReminder = window.setTimeout(() => {
                if (active) {
                    statusText.textContent =
                        "Still waiting for camera permission. Allow access in the browser prompt or site settings.";
                }
            }, 10000);
            let cameraStream;
            try {
                cameraStream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
                    video: {
                        width: { ideal: 640 },
                        height: { ideal: 480 },
                        facingMode: "user"
                    },
                    audio: false
                });
            } finally {
                window.clearTimeout(permissionReminder);
            }
            if (!active) {
                cameraStream.getTracks().forEach(track => track.stop());
                return;
            }
            statusText.textContent = "Camera permission granted. Starting camera preview...";
            stream = cameraStream;
            stream.getVideoTracks().forEach(track => {
                track.addEventListener("ended", () => {
                    interruptTest(
                        "Camera disconnected. Restart the test and grant camera access."
                    );
                }, { once: true });
            });
            video.srcObject = stream;
            await video.play();
            if (!active) return;
            stage.classList.add("eye-camera-active");
            statusText.textContent = "Camera ready. Starting assessment session...";

            const started = await requestJson(
                `/eye-assessment/${encodeURIComponent(assessmentId)}/smooth-pursuit/browser/start`,
                { method: "POST" }
            );
            sessionId = started.sessionId;
            if (!active) {
                await cancelServerSession(true);
                return;
            }
            statusText.textContent = "Assessment session ready. Connecting to GazeMap...";
            await initializeGazeMap();
            if (!active) return;
            statusText.textContent = "Calibrating. Follow each on-screen dot.";
            await calibrate();
            if (!active) return;

            statusText.textContent = "Calibration complete. Follow the moving target.";
            await wait(350);
            calibrationUI.hide();
            if (active) await runTracking();
        } catch (error) {
            active = false;
            stopCamera();
            currentStimulus = null;
            stage.classList.remove("eye-test-active");
            startButton.disabled = false;
            cancelButton.hidden = true;
            stageMessage.textContent =
                error.message || "The assessment could not be completed.";
            statusText.textContent =
                `Unable to complete the test: ${stageMessage.textContent}`;
            drawStage();
            if (sessionId) await cancelServerSession();
            await exitFullscreen();
        }
    }

    async function cancelTest() {
        if (!active) return;
        active = false;
        cancelButton.disabled = true;
        statusText.textContent = "Cancelling the assessment...";
        stopCamera();
        currentStimulus = null;
        stage.classList.remove("eye-test-active");
        drawStage();
        await cancelServerSession();
        cancelButton.hidden = true;
        cancelButton.disabled = false;
        startButton.disabled = false;
        statusText.textContent = "Assessment cancelled. Select Start Test to try again.";
        stageMessage.textContent = "Assessment cancelled.";
        await exitFullscreen();
    }

    startButton.addEventListener("click", startTest);
    cancelButton.addEventListener("click", cancelTest);

    document.addEventListener("fullscreenchange", () => {
        if (active && document.fullscreenElement !== runner) {
            interruptTest("Fullscreen was exited. Restart the assessment to continue.");
        } else if (document.fullscreenElement === runner) {
            resizeCanvas();
        }
    });
    document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", () => {
        if (active && document.hidden) {
            interruptTest(
                "The browser tab was hidden. Restart the assessment to keep tracking synchronized."
            );
        }
    });

    window.addEventListener("resize", resizeCanvas);
    window.addEventListener("pagehide", () => {
        active = false;
        stopCamera();
        if (sessionId) void cancelServerSession(true);
    });
    resizeCanvas();
});

const mongoose = require("mongoose");

const assessmentSchema = new mongoose.Schema({
    name: {
        type: String,
        required: true
    },

    email: {
        type: String,
        required: true,
        lowercase: true,
        trim: true
    }
}, {
    timestamps: true
});

module.exports = mongoose.model(
    "Assessment",
    assessmentSchema
);

const mongoose = require("mongoose");

const opennessSampleSchema = new mongoose.Schema(
    {
        timeSeconds: { type: Number, required: true },
        leftEAR: { type: Number, default: null },
        rightEAR: { type: Number, default: null },
        avgEAR: { type: Number, default: null },
        faceDetected: { type: Boolean, required: true },
        valid: { type: Boolean, required: true },
    },
    { _id: false }
);

const closureEventSchema = new mongoose.Schema(
    {
        startTimeSeconds: { type: Number, required: true },
        endTimeSeconds: { type: Number, required: true },
        durationMs: { type: Number, required: true },
        category: {
            type: String,
            enum: ["blink", "longer_closure", "prolonged_closure", "uncertain_incomplete"],
            required: true,
        },
    },
    { _id: false }
);

const blinkMonitoringResultSchema = new mongoose.Schema(
    {
        assessment: {
            type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
            ref: "Assessment",
            required: true,
            index: true,
        },
        email: { type: String, required: true, lowercase: true, trim: true },
        sessionId: { type: String, required: true, unique: true },
        assessmentType: {
            type: String,
            enum: ["blink_monitoring"],
            default: "blink_monitoring",
            required: true,
        },
        status: {
            type: String,
            enum: ["running", "completed", "incomplete", "failed"],
            default: "running",
            required: true,
        },
        message: { type: String, default: null },
        startedAt: { type: Date, default: Date.now },
        endedAt: { type: Date, default: null },
        durationSeconds: { type: Number, default: null },
        coordinateSpace: { type: String, default: "fullscreen_window_pixels" },
        screen: {
            widthPx: { type: Number, default: null },
            heightPx: { type: Number, default: null },
        },
        calibration: {
            available: { type: Boolean, default: false },
            method: { type: String, default: null },
            earThreshold: { type: Number, default: null },
            baselineEAR: { type: Number, default: null },
        },
        measurements: {
            totalBlinks: { type: Number, default: null },
            blinksPerMinute: { type: Number, default: null },
            avgBlinkDurationMs: { type: Number, default: null },
            medianBlinkDurationMs: { type: Number, default: null },
            minBlinkDurationMs: { type: Number, default: null },
            maxBlinkDurationMs: { type: Number, default: null },
            longestClosureMs: { type: Number, default: null },
            prolongedClosureCount: { type: Number, default: null },
            longerClosureCount: { type: Number, default: null },
            totalClosureEvents: { type: Number, default: null },
            validTrackingPct: { type: Number, default: null },
            totalFrames: { type: Number, default: null },
            validFrames: { type: Number, default: null },
            trackedSeconds: { type: Number, default: null },
            sampleRateHz: { type: Number, default: null },
        },
        sampleQuality: {
            earThreshold: { type: Number, default: null },
            baselineEAR: { type: Number, default: null },
            qualityNotes: { type: [String], default: [] },
        },
        observations: { type: [String], default: [] },
        samples: { type: [opennessSampleSchema], default: [] },
        blinkEvents: { type: [closureEventSchema], default: [] },
        closureEvents: { type: [closureEventSchema], default: [] },
    },
    { timestamps: true }
);

module.exports = mongoose.model("BlinkMonitoringResult", blinkMonitoringResultSchema);

const mongoose = require("mongoose");

const targetSchema = new mongoose.Schema(
    {
        id: { type: String, required: true },
        side: { type: String, enum: ["left", "right"], default: null },
        xPx: { type: Number, required: true },
        yPx: { type: Number, required: true },
        cueOnsetAtSeconds: { type: Number, default: null },
        appearedAtSeconds: { type: Number, default: null },
        disappearedAtSeconds: { type: Number, default: null },
        averageGazeToTargetDistancePx: { type: Number, default: null },
        fixationCount: { type: Number, default: null },
        fixationDetected: { type: Boolean, default: null },
        fixationDurationMs: { type: Number, default: null },
        validSampleCount: { type: Number, default: null },
        endpointErrorPx: { type: Number, default: null },
        acquired: { type: Boolean, default: null },
        responseLatencyMs: { type: Number, default: null },
    },
    { _id: false }
);

const sampleSchema = new mongoose.Schema(
    {
        timeSeconds: { type: Number, required: true },
        xPx: { type: Number, default: null },
        yPx: { type: Number, default: null },
        targetXPx: { type: Number, default: null },
        targetYPx: { type: Number, default: null },
        valid: { type: Boolean, required: true },
        onScreen: { type: Boolean, required: true },
        distanceMm: { type: Number, default: null },
        reason: { type: String, default: null },
    },
    { _id: false }
);

const eyeAssessmentResultSchema = new mongoose.Schema(
    {
        assessment: {
            type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
            ref: "Assessment",
            required: true,
            index: true,
        },
        email: { type: String, required: true, lowercase: true, trim: true },
        sessionId: { type: String, required: true, unique: true },
        assessmentType: {
            type: String,
            enum: ["saccadic", "smooth_pursuit", "blink_monitoring", "memory_tracking"],
            required: true,
        },
        status: {
            type: String,
            enum: ["running", "completed", "incomplete", "failed"],
            default: "running",
            required: true,
        },
        message: { type: String, default: null },
        startedAt: { type: Date, default: Date.now },
        endedAt: { type: Date, default: null },
        durationSeconds: { type: Number, default: null },
        coordinateSpace: { type: String, default: null },
        screen: {
            widthPx: { type: Number, default: null },
            heightPx: { type: Number, default: null },
        },
        calibration: {
            available: { type: Boolean, default: false },
            mappingMethod: { type: String, default: null },
            calibrationPointCount: { type: Number, default: 0 },
        },
        measurements: {
            totalSamples: { type: Number, default: null },
            validGazeSamples: { type: Number, default: null },
            invalidGazeSamples: { type: Number, default: null },
            onScreenGazeSamples: { type: Number, default: null },
            numberOfTargetsDisplayed: { type: Number, default: null },
            fixationCount: { type: Number, default: null },
            averageFixationDurationMs: { type: Number, default: null },
            averageGazeToTargetDistancePx: { type: Number, default: null },
            targetFixationConsistencyPct: { type: Number, default: null },
            missingSamplePercentage: { type: Number, default: null },
            totalTrials: { type: Number, default: null },
            trialsWithEnoughGazeSamples: { type: Number, default: null },
            targetsWithEndpointError: { type: Number, default: null },
            successfulTargetAcquisitions: { type: Number, default: null },
            targetAcquisitionRatePct: { type: Number, default: null },
            meanResponseLatencyMs: { type: Number, default: null },
            medianResponseLatencyMs: { type: Number, default: null },
            meanEndpointErrorPx: { type: Number, default: null },

            // Smooth pursuit fields (was missing — this was the bug)
            meanPositionErrorPx: { type: Number, default: null },
            rmsePositionErrorPx: { type: Number, default: null },
            trackingAccuracyPct: { type: Number, default: null },
            pursuitGain: { type: Number, default: null },
            catchUpSaccades: { type: Number, default: null },
            trackingContinuityPct: { type: Number, default: null },
        },
        sampleQuality: {
            sampleRateHz: { type: Number, default: null },
            validSampleFraction: { type: Number, default: null },
            fixationAnalysisSupported: { type: Boolean, default: null },
            targetAcquisitionSupported: { type: Boolean, default: null },
            latencyAnalysisSupported: { type: Boolean, default: null },
            fixationMethod: { type: String, default: null },
            dispersionThresholdDegrees: { type: Number, default: null },
            minimumFixationDurationMs: { type: Number, default: null },
            minimumFixationSamples: { type: Number, default: null },
            targetFixationRadiusDegrees: { type: Number, default: null },
            targetRadiusDegrees: { type: Number, default: null },
            targetsWithSufficientGazeSamples: { type: Number, default: null },
            thresholdPx: { type: Number, default: null },
            qualityNotes: { type: [String], default: [] },
        },
        metricExplanations: {
            averageGazeToTargetDistancePx: { type: String, default: null },
            averageFixationDurationMs: { type: String, default: null },
            targetFixationConsistencyPct: { type: String, default: null },
            fixationAnalysis: { type: String, default: null },
            responseLatency: { type: String, default: null },
            endpointError: { type: String, default: null },
            targetAcquisition: { type: String, default: null },
        },
        targets: { type: [targetSchema], default: [] },
        samples: { type: [sampleSchema], default: [] },
    },
    { timestamps: true }
);

module.exports = mongoose.model("EyeAssessmentResult", eyeAssessmentResultSchema);

const mongoose = require("mongoose");

const gazePointSchema = new mongoose.Schema(
    {
        seconds: {
            type: Number,
            required: true
        },
        x_px: {
            type: Number,
            required: true
        },
        y_px: {
            type: Number,
            required: true
        }
    },
    { _id: false }
);

const eyeTrackingSchema = new mongoose.Schema(
    {
        assessment: {
            type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
            ref: "Assessment",
            required: true,
            index: true
        },

        email: {
            type: String,
            required: true,
            lowercase: true
        },

        session: {
            type: String,
            required: true
        },

        stimulus: {
            type: String,
            default: "test-image.jpg"
        },

        sampleCount: {
            type: Number,
            required: true
        },

        durationSeconds: {
            type: Number,
            required: true
        },

        coordinateSpace: {
            type: String,
            default: "heatmap_pixels"
        },

        calibrationStatus: {
            type: String,
            enum: ["not_started", "completed"],
            default: "not_started"
        },

        calibrationPointCount: {
            type: Number,
            default: 0
        },

        heatmapFilename: {
            type: String,
            default: null
        },

        points: {
            type: [gazePointSchema],
            default: []
        },

        source: {
            type: String,
            default: "GazeMap"
        }
    },
    {
        timestamps: true
    }
);

    "EyeTracking",

const mongoose = require("mongoose");

const gazeSampleSchema = new mongoose.Schema(
    {
        timeSeconds: { type: Number, required: true },
        xPx: { type: Number, default: null },
        yPx: { type: Number, default: null },
        valid: { type: Boolean, required: true },
        phase: { type: String, enum: ["encoding", "recognition", "retention"], required: true },
        imageId: { type: String, default: null },
    },
    { _id: false }
);

const memoryEyeTrackingSchema = new mongoose.Schema(
    {
        assessment: { type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: "Assessment", required: true, index: true },
        email: { type: String, required: true, lowercase: true, trim: true },
        sessionId: { type: String, required: true, unique: true },
        status: { type: String, enum: ["running", "completed", "incomplete", "failed"], default: "running" },
        message: { type: String, default: null },
        startedAt: { type: Date, default: Date.now },
        endedAt: { type: Date, default: null },
        durationSeconds: { type: Number, default: null },
        coordinateSpace: { type: String, default: null },
        screen: {
            widthPx: { type: Number, default: null },
            heightPx: { type: Number, default: null },
        },
        calibration: {
            available: { type: Boolean, default: false },
            mappingMethod: { type: String, default: null },
            calibrationPointCount: { type: Number, default: 0 },
        },
        
        measurements: {
            recognitionAccuracy: { type: Number, default: null },
            hits: { type: Number, default: null },
            misses: { type: Number, default: null },
            correctRejections: { type: Number, default: null },
            falseAlarms: { type: Number, default: null },
            meanResponseTimeMs: { type: Number, default: null },
            dPrime: { type: Number, default: null },
            
            meanEncodingFixationMs: { type: Number, default: null },
            meanRecognitionFixationMs: { type: Number, default: null },
        },
        
        sampleQuality: {
            totalSamples: { type: Number, default: null },
            validSamples: { type: Number, default: null },
            validTrackingPct: { type: Number, default: null },
            qualityNotes: { type: [String], default: [] },
        },
        
        trials: [{
            imageId: String,
            phase: String, // "encoding" or "recognition"
            isOld: Boolean,
            userResponse: String, // "seen" or "not_seen"
            isCorrect: Boolean,
            responseTimeMs: Number,
            fixationCount: Number,
            fixationDurationMs: Number
        }],
        
        samples: { type: [gazeSampleSchema], default: [] },
    },
    { timestamps: true }
);

module.exports = mongoose.model("MemoryEyeTracking", memoryEyeTrackingSchema);

================================================================================
FILE: C:\Users\surendra purohit\Documents\clean\ml-service\main.py
================================================================================

from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional, Any

import numpy as np

import fastapi  # pyright: ignore[reportMissingImports]
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

from sklearn.linear_model import LinearRegression
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
from sklearn.ensemble import IsolationForest


app = fastapi.FastAPI(
    title="BrainGuard AI ML Service",
    description="Experimental cognitive performance trend analysis",
    version="1.0.0"
)


# ==========================================
# INPUT MODELS
# ==========================================

class AssessmentRecord(BaseModel):
    createdAt: Optional[datetime] = None
    memoryCorrect: int = Field(ge=0)
    memoryTotal: int = Field(default=5, gt=0)
    attentionCorrect: bool
    reactionMs: float = Field(gt=0)


class AnalysisRequest(BaseModel):
    history: list[AssessmentRecord]
    health_profile: Optional[dict[str, Any]] = None


# ==========================================
# FEATURE ENGINEERING
# ==========================================

def prepare_features(history):

    features = []

    for item in history:

        memory_percentage = (
            item.memoryCorrect / item.memoryTotal
        ) * 100

        attention_score = (
            1 if item.attentionCorrect else 0
        )

        reaction_time = np.log1p(item.reactionMs)

        features.append([
            memory_percentage,
            attention_score,
            reaction_time
        ])

    return np.array(features, dtype=float)


# ==========================================
# BASELINE CALCULATION
# ==========================================

def calculate_baseline(history):

    baseline_sessions = history[:3]

    features = prepare_features(baseline_sessions)

    return {
        "sessions": len(baseline_sessions),

        "memoryPct": round(
            float(np.median(features[:, 0])), 2
        ),

        "attentionRatePct": round(
            float(np.median(features[:, 1])) * 100, 2
        ),

        "reactionMs": round(
            float(np.median([
                item.reactionMs
                for item in baseline_sessions
            ])), 2
        )
    }


# ==========================================
# BASELINE COMPARISON
# ==========================================

def compare_with_baseline(baseline, latest):

    memory_pct = (
        latest.memoryCorrect / latest.memoryTotal
    ) * 100

    attention_pct = (
        100 if latest.attentionCorrect else 0
    )

    memory_change = (
        memory_pct - baseline["memoryPct"]
    )

    attention_change = (
        attention_pct - baseline["attentionRatePct"]
    )

    reaction_change = (
        latest.reactionMs - baseline["reactionMs"]
    )

    reaction_change_pct = (
        reaction_change / baseline["reactionMs"] * 100
        if baseline["reactionMs"] > 0 else 0
    )

    return {
        "memoryPctPoints": round(memory_change, 2),

        "attentionPoints": round(attention_change, 2),

        "reactionMs": round(reaction_change, 2),

        "reactionPercent": round(reaction_change_pct, 2)
    }


# ==========================================
# LINEAR REGRESSION TREND ANALYSIS
# ==========================================

def calculate_trends(history):

    if len(history) < 3:
        return None

    features = prepare_features(history)

    x = np.arange(len(history)).reshape(-1, 1)

    def get_slope(values):

        model = LinearRegression()
        model.fit(x, values)

        return float(model.coef_[0])

    memory_slope = get_slope(features[:, 0])

    attention_slope = get_slope(features[:, 1])

    reaction_slope = get_slope(
        np.array([
            item.reactionMs for item in history
        ])
    )

    def classify(value, threshold):

        if value > threshold:
            return "upward"

        if value < -threshold:
            return "downward"

        return "stable"

    return {
        "memory": {
            "slopePerSession": round(memory_slope, 2),
            "direction": classify(memory_slope, 1.0)
        },

        "attention": {
            "slopePerSession": round(attention_slope, 3),
            "direction": classify(attention_slope, 0.03)
        },

        "reaction": {
            "slopeMsPerSession": round(reaction_slope, 2),
            "direction": (
                "slower" if reaction_slope > 10
                else "faster" if reaction_slope < -10
                else "stable"
            )
        }
    }


# ==========================================
# ISOLATION FOREST ANOMALY DETECTION
# ==========================================

def detect_anomaly(history):

    # Need at least 8 previous sessions
    # plus one latest session.

    if len(history) < 9:

        return {
            "status": "not_enough_history",
            "isUnusual": None,
            "score": None,
            "message": (
                "At least 9 sessions are required "
                "for experimental anomaly detection."
            )
        }

    features = prepare_features(history)

    previous_sessions = features[:-1]

    latest_session = features[-1].reshape(1, -1)

    scaler = StandardScaler()

    previous_scaled = scaler.fit_transform(
        previous_sessions
    )

    latest_scaled = scaler.transform(
        latest_session
    )

    model = IsolationForest(
        n_estimators=200,
        contamination=0.15,
        random_state=42
    )

    model.fit(previous_scaled)

    prediction = model.predict(latest_scaled)

    score = model.decision_function(latest_scaled)[0]

    is_unusual = bool(prediction[0] == -1)

    return {
        "status": "completed",
        "isUnusual": is_unusual,
        "score": round(float(score), 4),

        "message": (
            "This session differs from previous personal measurements."
            if is_unusual
            else "No unusual session pattern was detected."
        )
    }


# ==========================================
# HEALTH CONTEXT
# ==========================================

def get_health_context(profile):

    if not profile:

        return {
            "available": False
        }

    return {
        "available": True,
        "sleepHours": profile.get("sleepHours"),
        "stressLevel": profile.get("stressLevel"),
        "exerciseMinutes": profile.get("exerciseMinutes"),
        "familyHistory": profile.get("familyHistory")
    }


# ==========================================
# MAIN ANALYSIS ENDPOINT
# ==========================================

@app.post("/analyze")
def analyze_brain_health(request: AnalysisRequest):

    history = sorted(
        request.history,
        key=lambda item: item.createdAt or datetime.min
    )

    if not history:

        return {
            "success": False,
            "message": "No assessment history available."
        }

    # Current session
    latest = history[-1]

    latest_result = {
        "memoryPct": round(
            latest.memoryCorrect / latest.memoryTotal * 100, 2
        ),

        "attentionCorrect": latest.attentionCorrect,

        "reactionMs": round(latest.reactionMs, 2)
    }

    # Baseline
    baseline = None
    comparison = None

    if len(history) >= 3:

        baseline = calculate_baseline(history)

    # Compare only sessions AFTER the first 3 baseline sessions
    if len(history) >= 4:

        comparison = compare_with_baseline(
            baseline,
            latest
        )

    # Trend
    trends = calculate_trends(history)

    # Anomaly detection
    anomaly = detect_anomaly(history)

    # Reported health context
    health_context = get_health_context(
        request.health_profile
    )

    return {
        "success": True,

        "sessionCount": len(history),

        "baselineAvailable": baseline is not None,

        "baseline": baseline,

        "latest": latest_result,

        "comparison": comparison,

        "trends": trends,

        "anomaly": anomaly,

        "healthContext": health_context,

        "disclaimer": (
            "Experimental performance analysis only. "
            "Not a diagnosis or clinically validated screening result."
        )
    }


# ==========================================
# HEALTH CHECK
# ==========================================

@app.get("/")
def home():

    return {
        "message": "BrainGuard AI ML Service is running",
        "status": "healthy"
    }

const dns = require("node:dns");

// Configure DNS before Mongoose is loaded.
dns.setServers(["8.8.8.8", "1.1.1.1"]);

const mongoose = require("mongoose");

const connectDB = async () => {
    try {
        await mongoose.connect(process.env.MONGODB_URI);

        console.log("MongoDB connected successfully");
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("MongoDB connection failed:", error.message);
        process.exit(1);
    }
};

module.exports = connectDB;
